  • 本期季节性流感病毒研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • 值得注意的是,LNP@NP-PROTAC#4在体外和体内均展现出显著的抗病毒药物活性。 [10.1080/21505594.2026.2721813]。
  • 与 AlphaMissense 的比较揭示了预测非结构化 N 末端基序和脂质嵌入域内变体影响的系统局限性。 [10.1038/s42003-026-10800-0]。
  • 克隆 4F02 识别了传播数十年的人类甲型流感 (H3N2) 病毒的 HA。它能中和多种 H3N2 病毒,表现出微弱的血凝抑制作用,阻断 HA 介导的融合活性,激活 Fc 受体介导的信号传导,并保护小鼠免受致命攻击。冷冻电镜显示,4F02 靶向位于头-茎界面处的 HA 头基部,跨越抗原位点 C、D 和 E。在人类血清中检测到了针对类似表位的抗体,尽管水平较低。
  • 研究在分支K变异株的血凝素(HA)蛋白中识别出多个突变,包括抗原位点A、B、D区域及受体结合位点(RBS)的关键替换[A1]。 [10.1002/mco2.70953]。

  • Importantly, LNP@NP-PROTAC#4 demonstrated potent antiviral activity both in vitro and in vivo. [10.1080/21505594.2026.2721813].
  • Comparison with AlphaMissense reveals systematic limitations in predicting the effects of variants within unstructured N-terminal motifs and lipid-embedded domains. [10.1038/s42003-026-10800-0].
  • Clone 4F02 recognised the HA of human influenza A (H3N2) viruses that circulated across multiple decades.
  • The study identified multiple mutations in the hemagglutinin (HA) protein of the subclade K variants, including key substitutions in regions of antigenic sites A, B, and D, as well as the receptor binding site (RBS) [A1].

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: MedComm  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-08-19  |  DOI: 10.1002/mco2.70953  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 7, 9, e70953
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

新兴 季节性流感 A (H3N2) 分支 K 的适应性优势:增强的受体结合能力和免疫逃逸潜力

Adaptive Advantage of the Emerging Seasonal Influenza A (H3N2) Subclade K: Enhanced Receptor Binding Capacity and Immune Escape Potential
作者： Lulu Zhang, Tingbi Zhao, Xiaoqian Pan, Zhaomin Feng, Daitao Zhang, Quanyi Wang, Peng Yang
摘要中文翻译新兴流感变异株继续在全球引发流感流行。值得注意的是,甲型H3N2流感病毒分支K(J.2.4.1)目前是2025-2026年北半球流感季的优势株,正在亚洲、欧洲和北美快速传播[1]。这些变异株在血凝素(HA)蛋白中表现出多个突变,包括抗原位点A、B、D区域及受体结合位点(RBS)的关键替换(图1A)。值得注意的是,第135位(丧失)和第144位(获得)的糖基化改变可能有助于其增强适应性。分支K的快速传播,以及日本、英国(UK)和美国(USA)等国早期报告的流感病例,已促使公共卫生机构优先更新疫苗株和抗病毒药物策略[2,3]。然而,新兴分支K的生物学特征仍不清楚。本研究从全球共享流感数据倡议(GISAID)平台检索了属于分支J(2a.3a.1)的甲型H3N2流感病毒所有可用HA序列。这些序列在地理上源自亚洲、欧洲和北美,时间跨度为2020年6月6日至2026年1月16日。共获得62,080条HA序列,其中包含2025-2026流感季的11,014条序列。在这些序列中,9,795条(88.93%)被归类为分支K。研究随机从三个地区的每个分支(J、J.1、J.2、J.2.2、J.2.4、K)中各选取5条病毒序列,共计90条病毒序列用于生物学特征研究。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
新出现的流感变种继续在全球范围内引发流感浪潮。值得注意的是,甲型流感 (H3N2) K 亚支 (J.2.4.1) 目前是 2025-2026 年北半球流感季节的主导毒株,在亚洲、欧洲和北美迅速传播 [A1]。
设计与对象
该研究从GISAID平台检索了属于J分支(2a.3a.1)的甲型流感(H3N2)病毒的所有可用HA序列,其地理来源来自亚洲、欧洲和北美,时间跨度为2020年6月6日至2026年1月16日[A1]。总共获得了 62,080 个 HA 序列,其中包括 2025-2026 年流感季节的 11,014 个序列。其中,9,795(88.93%)被归类为K亚支。
核心方法
研究随机从三个地区的每个分支(J、J.1、J.2、J.2.2、J.2.4、K)中各选取5条病毒序列,共计90条病毒序列用于生物学特征研究[A1]。
主要结果
研究在分支K变异株的血凝素(HA)蛋白中识别出多个突变,包括抗原位点A、B、D区域及受体结合位点(RBS)的关键替换[A1]。
解释与创新
作者将这些突变解释为可能有助于分支K增强适应性,这或许能解释其快速传播和免疫逃逸潜力[A1]。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了更新疫苗株和抗病毒药物策略的必要性,以应对分支K的进化特征,该分支目前是2025-2026流感季的优势株[A1]。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其依赖 GISAID 平台的序列数据而受到限制,该平台可能无法完全代表所有流行菌株。由于分析的观察性质,证据的强度中等[A1]。

Adaptive Advantage of the Emerging Seasonal Influenza A (H3N2) Subclade K: Enhanced Receptor Binding Capacity and Immune Escape Potential

Authors: Lulu Zhang, Tingbi Zhao, Xiaoqian Pan, Zhaomin Feng, Daitao Zhang, Quanyi Wang, Peng Yang
Original abstract (English)
Emerging influenza variants continue to drive waves of flu globally.Notably, the influenza A (H3N2) subclade K (J.2.4.1) is currently the dominant strain during the 2025-2026 Northern Hemisphere influenza season, spreading rapidly across Asia, Europe, and North America [ 1 ].These variants exhibit multiple mutations in the hemagglutinin (HA) protein, including key substitutions in regions of antigenic sites A, B, and D, as well as the receptor binding site (RBS) (Figure 1A ).Notably, glycosylation changes at positions 135 (loss) and 144 (gain) may contribute to its enhanced adaptability.The rapid spread of subclade K, along with early reports of flu outbreaks in countries such as Japan, the United Kingdom (UK) and the United States (USA), has prompted public health agencies to prioritize surveillance and vaccine updates [ 2,3 ].However, the biological characteristics of the newly emerging subclade K remain unclear.In this study, we retrieved all available HA sequences of influenza A (H3N2) virus belonging to clade J (2a.3a.1) from the Global Initiative on Sharing All Influenza Data (GISAID) platform.These sequences were geographically sourced from Asia, Europe, and North America, spanning the period from June 6, 2020 to January 16, 2026.A total of 62,080 HA sequences were obtained, including 11,014 sequences from the 2025-2026 influenza season.Among these, 9,795 (88.93%) were classified as subclade K.We randomly selected 5 viral sequences from each subclade (J, J.1, J.2, J.2.2, J.2.4, K) in each region of the three areas, totaling 90 viral sequences for the study of biological characteristics.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Emerging influenza variants continue to drive waves of flu globally. Notably, the influenza A (H3N2) subclade K (J.2.4.1) is currently the dominant strain during the 2025-2026 Northern Hemisphere influenza season, spreading rapidly across Asia, Europe, and North America [A1].
Design and population
The study retrieved all available HA sequences of influenza A (H3N2) virus belonging to clade J (2a.3a.1) from the GISAID platform, geographically sourced from Asia, Europe, and North America, spanning the period from June 6, 2020 to January 16, 2026 [A1]. A total of 62,080 HA sequences were obtained, including 11,014 sequences from the 2025-2026 influenza season. Among these, 9,795 (88.93%) were classified as subclade K.
Core methods
The study randomly selected 5 viral sequences from each subclade (J, J.1, J.2, J.2.2, J.2.4, K) in each region of the three areas, totaling 90 viral sequences for the study of biological characteristics [A1].
Main results
The study identified multiple mutations in the hemagglutinin (HA) protein of the subclade K variants, including key substitutions in regions of antigenic sites A, B, and D, as well as the receptor binding site (RBS) [A1].
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted these mutations as potentially contributing to the enhanced adaptability of subclade K, which may explain its rapid spread and immune escape potential [A1].
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the need for updated surveillance and vaccine strategies to address the evolving characteristics of subclade K, which is currently the dominant strain in the 2025-2026 influenza season [A1].
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on sequence data from the GISAID platform, which may not fully represent all circulating strains. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the observational nature of the analysis [A1].

巴西甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09的系统地理动态与空间连通性

Phylogeographic Dynamics and Spatial Connectivity of Influenza A(H3N2) in Brazil
作者： Wanderley Dias das Chagas Junior, Amanda Mendes Silva Cruz, Luana Soares Barbagelata, Jessylene de Almeida Ferreira, Edivaldo Costa Sousa Junior, Edna Maria Acunã de Sousa Fillizola, Wagner Davy Lucas Barreto Júnior, Tulio de Lima Campos, Terezinha Maria de Paiva, Katia Corrêa de Oliveira Santos
摘要中文翻译甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09持续发生抗原漂移与遗传突变,需要持续开展分子流行病学监测以理解其流行与地理传播。本研究调查了2011年至2019年间在巴西流行的甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09的流行病学、进化与系统地理动态。研究分析了巴西北部与东北部的流行病学监测数据,并结合本研究产生的血凝素(HA)基因序列及从GISAID EpiFlu数据库检索的序列进行分析。开展系统发育与贝叶斯系统地理学分析以表征遗传多样性与病毒扩散。在23,278份呼吸道样本中,1,003份(4.31%)甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09检测阳性,季节性高峰主要出现在流行病学周第13至16周。系统发育分析显示病毒谱系持续替换,2015年后3C.2a衍生分支占主导地位。系统地理重建识别出多次国际输入巴西事件,以及巴西各地区间的广泛病毒流动,其中东南部巴西作为国家连通性的主要枢纽。北部与东北部巴西主要作为受体地区,同时也为后续病毒传播做出贡献。研究结果凸显甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09在巴西流行的相互连通性,并强调整合基因组与流行病学监测以理解流感动态的重要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在调查巴西甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09的流行病学、进化与系统地理动态,以理解其流行与地理传播,因甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09持续发生抗原漂移与遗传突变,需要持续开展分子流行病学监测。
设计与对象
本研究分析了巴西北部与东北部的流行病学监测数据,并结合本研究产生的血凝素(HA)基因序列及从GISAID EpiFlu数据库检索的序列。研究聚焦于2011年至2019年间收集的呼吸道样本。
核心方法
开展系统发育与贝叶斯系统地理学分析以表征遗传多样性与病毒扩散。分析方法包括血凝素(HA)基因序列分析,并将其与流行病学数据整合。
主要结果
在23,278份呼吸道样本中,1,003份(4.31%)甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09检测阳性,季节性高峰主要出现在流行病学周第13至16周。系统发育分析显示病毒谱系持续替换,2015年后3C.2a衍生分支占主导地位。
解释与创新
研究结果展示甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09在巴西流行的相互连通性,其中东南部巴西作为国家连通性的主要枢纽。本研究为甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09的空间动态提供了新颖见解。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调整合基因组与流行病学监测以更好地理解流感动态的重要性,可为制定有针对性的公共卫生干预措施及提升未来疫情应对能力提供参考。
局限与证据强度
本研究依赖流行病学监测数据与基因组序列,可能在捕捉完整病毒扩散模式方面存在局限性。然而,多源数据的整合支持研究结论具有中等可信度。

Phylogeographic Dynamics and Spatial Connectivity of Influenza A(H3N2) in Brazil

Authors: Wanderley Dias das Chagas Junior, Amanda Mendes Silva Cruz, Luana Soares Barbagelata, Jessylene de Almeida Ferreira, Edivaldo Costa Sousa Junior, Edna Maria Acunã de Sousa Fillizola, Wagner Davy Lucas Barreto Júnior, Tulio de Lima Campos, Terezinha Maria de Paiva, Katia Corrêa de Oliveira Santos
Original abstract (English)
Influenza A(H3N2) viruses show continuous antigenic and genetic evolution, requiring sustained molecular surveillance to understand their circulation and geographic spread. This study investigated the epidemiological, evolutionary, and phylogeographic dynamics of A(H3N2) viruses circulating in Brazil between 2011 and 2019. Epidemiological surveillance data from Northern and Northeastern Brazil were analyzed together with hemagglutinin (HA) gene sequences generated in this study and sequences retrieved from GISAID EpiFlu database. Phylogenetic and Bayesian phylogeographic analyses were performed to characterize genetic diversity and viral dispersal. Among 23,278 respiratory samples, 1,003 (4.31%) were positive for A(H3N2), with seasonal peaks occurring mainly between epidemiological weeks 13 and 16. Phylogenetic analyses revealed successive lineage replacement, with predominance of 3C.2a-derived clades after 2015. Phylogeographic reconstruction identified multiple international introductions into Brazil and extensive viral movement among Brazilian regions, with Southeastern Brazil acting as the main hub of national connectivity. Northern and Northeastern Brazil participated predominantly as recipient regions while also contributing to onward viral dissemination. These findings highlight the interconnected nature of A(H3N2) circulation in Brazil and reinforce the importance of integrating genomic and epidemiological surveillance to understand influenza dynamics.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to investigate the epidemiological, evolutionary, and phylogeographic dynamics of A(H3N2) viruses in Brazil to understand their circulation and geographic spread, as influenza A(H3N2) viruses show continuous antigenic and genetic evolution, requiring sustained molecular surveillance.
Design and population
The study analyzed epidemiological surveillance data from Northern and Northeastern Brazil alongside hemagglutinin (HA) gene sequences generated in this study and retrieved from the GISAID EpiFlu database. It focused on respiratory samples collected between 2011 and 2019.
Core methods
Phylogenetic and Bayesian phylogeographic analyses were conducted to characterize genetic diversity and viral dispersal. These methods involved analyzing HA gene sequences and integrating them with epidemiological data.
Main results
Among 23,278 respiratory samples, 1,003 (4.31%) were positive for A(H3N2), with seasonal peaks occurring mainly between epidemiological weeks 13 and 16. Phylogenetic analyses revealed successive lineage replacement, with predominance of 3C.2a-derived clades after 2015.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate the interconnected nature of A(H3N2) circulation in Brazil, with Southeastern Brazil acting as the main hub of national connectivity. This study provides novel insights into the spatial dynamics of influenza A(H3N2) viruses.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results highlight the importance of integrating genomic and epidemiological surveillance to better understand influenza dynamics, which can inform targeted public health interventions and improve preparedness for future outbreaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study relies on surveillance data and genomic sequences, which may have limitations in capturing complete viral dispersal patterns. However, the integration of multiple data sources supports a moderate confidence in the findings.

Journal: eBioMedicine  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-08-23  |  DOI: 10.1016/j.ebiom.2026.106450  |  Date basis: online_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 131, 106450
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

针对流感甲型H3N2流感病毒病毒HA头部基部保守表位的人源单克隆抗体表征

Characterisation of a human monoclonal antibody targeting a conserved epitope at the base of the HA head of influenza A(H3N2) virus
作者： Seiya Yamayoshi, Taiki Hamabata, Yuka Inoue, Maki Kiso, Moe Okuda, Hiroaki Akasaka, Wataru Shihoya, Takeaki Imamura, Yukimasa Matsuzawa, Daisuke Jubishi
摘要中文翻译背景:多种广谱人源单克隆抗体(mAb)已针对甲型流感病毒血凝素(HA)的保守表位展开研究,这些表位可跨越不同HA亚型或在同一亚型内保守。大多数异亚型mAb靶向HA茎部、受体结合位点(RBS)或三聚体界面。尽管至少有三种H3特异性mAb识别这些区域外的表位,但H3-HA保守表位的整体格局仍未完全明确。本研究旨在识别并表征H3-HA上的保守表位,为开发能够抵御抗原变异的疫苗提供依据。方法:筛选既往报道的H3-HA反应性人源mAb,以识别识别保守表位的mAb。候选克隆034-10040 4F02(4F02)在体外接受中和活性、血凝抑制活性及HA介导的融合抑制活性和Fc受体介导的效应功能评估,并在致死性小鼠攻击模型中评估其保护效果。通过冷冻电子显微镜技术阐明4F02结合的结构基础。筛选人血清中针对类似表位的抗体。结果:克隆4F02识别跨越数十年流行的甲型H3N2流感病毒的HA。其可中和多种H3N2病毒,血凝抑制活性较弱,但可阻断HA介导的融合活性,激活Fc受体介导的信号传导,并在小鼠致死性攻击中提供保护。冷冻电子显微镜显示,4F02靶向HA头部基部的头-茎界面,该界面跨越抗原位点C、D和E。人血清中检测到针对类似表位的抗体,但水平较低。解读:对4F02表位的表征揭示了H3-HA头-茎界面这一先前被低估的脆弱位点。该发现拓展了对保守表位的认知,并为开发能够抵御抗原变异的流感疫苗提供了靶标。资助:本研究由日本医疗研究开发机构、JSPS KAKENHI及美国国立过敏症和传染病研究所资助。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在识别并表征H3-HA上的保守表位,为开发能够抵御抗原变异的疫苗提供信息。多种广泛反应性人源单克隆抗体(mAb)已针对甲型流感病毒血凝素(HA)并识别出跨亚型或亚型内的保守表位。
设计与对象
该研究筛选了一组先前报道的针对H3-HA的反应性人源单克隆抗体,以识别能够识别保守表位的抗体。候选克隆4F02在体外被评估了中和活性、血凝抑制活性、HA介导的融合抑制活性以及Fc受体介导的效应功能,并在致死性小鼠攻击模型中评估了其保护效果。
核心方法
我们筛选了一组先前报道的针对H3血凝素(HA)的反应性人单克隆抗体(mAb),以识别识别保守表位的mAb。使用冷冻电子显微镜确定了4F02结合的结构基础。筛选了人血清以检测针对类似表位的抗体。
主要结果
克隆 4F02 识别了传播数十年的人类甲型流感 (H3N2) 病毒的 HA。它能中和多种 H3N2 病毒,表现出微弱的血凝抑制作用,阻断 HA 介导的融合活性,激活 Fc 受体介导的信号传导,并保护小鼠免受致命攻击。冷冻电镜显示,4F02 靶向位于头-茎界面处的 HA 头基部,跨越抗原位点 C、D 和 E。在人类血清中检测到了针对类似表位的抗体,尽管水平较低。
解释与创新
对4F02表位的特征分析揭示了H3-HA头部茎部接口处一个此前未被充分重视的保守性位点。该发现拓展了对保守表位的认知,并为开发针对季节性流感的疫苗疫苗提供了靶标,该疫苗对抗原变异具有更强的适应性。
科研与公卫意义
这一发现为开发对抗原变化具有弹性的流感 疫苗 提供了一个潜在靶标,这可以改善针对 季节性流感 暴发 的公共卫生准备。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其依赖体外和小鼠模型数据而受到限制,这些数据可能无法完全代表人类 免疫应答。证据是强有力的,因为它通过功能验证直接识别和表征了 H3-HA 上的保守表位。

Characterisation of a human monoclonal antibody targeting a conserved epitope at the base of the HA head of influenza A(H3N2) virus

Authors: Seiya Yamayoshi, Taiki Hamabata, Yuka Inoue, Maki Kiso, Moe Okuda, Hiroaki Akasaka, Wataru Shihoya, Takeaki Imamura, Yukimasa Matsuzawa, Daisuke Jubishi
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Numerous broadly reactive human monoclonal antibodies (mAbs) against the haemagglutinin (HA) of influenza A viruses have recognised conserved epitopes across HA subtypes or within subtypes. Most heterosubtypic mAbs target the HA stem, the receptor-binding site (RBS), or the trimeric interface. Although at least three H3-specific mAbs recognise epitopes outside these regions, the overall landscape of conserved H3-specific epitopes remains incompletely understood. This study aimed to identify and characterise conserved epitopes on H3-HA to inform the development of vaccines resilient to antigenic change. METHODS: We screened a panel of previously reported H3-HA-reactive human mAbs to identify mAbs recognising conserved epitopes. The candidate clone, 034-10040 4F02 (4F02), was evaluated for neutralising, haemagglutination inhibiting, and HA-mediated fusion-inhibitory, Fc receptor-mediated effector functions in vitro, and for protective efficacy in a lethal mouse challenge model. Cryo-electron microscopy was used to define the structural basis of 4F02 binding. Human sera were screened for antibodies targeting similar epitopes. FINDINGS: Clone 4F02 recognised the HA of human influenza A (H3N2) viruses that circulated across multiple decades. It neutralised multiple H3N2 viruses, exhibited weak haemagglutination inhibition, blocked HA-mediated fusion activity, activated Fc receptor-mediated signalling, and protected mice against lethal challenge. Cryo-electron microscopy revealed that 4F02 targets the base of the HA head at a head-stem interface spanning antigenic sites C, D, and E. Antibodies targeting similar epitopes were detected, albeit at a low level, in human sera. INTERPRETATION: Characterisation of the 4F02 epitope reveals a previously underappreciated site of vulnerability at the H3-HA head-stem interface. This finding expands our understanding of conserved epitopes and provides a target for the development of influenza vaccines resilient to antigenic change. FUNDING: This work was supported by the Japan Agency for Medical Research and Development, JSPS KAKENHI, the National Institutes of Allergy and Infectious Diseases.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to identify and characterise conserved epitopes on H3-HA to inform the development of vaccines resilient to antigenic change. Numerous broadly reactive human monoclonal antibodies (mAbs) against the haemagglutinin (HA) of influenza A viruses have recognised conserved epitopes across HA subtypes or within subtypes.
Design and population
The study involved screening a panel of previously reported H3-HA-reactive human monoclonal antibodies to identify those recognising conserved epitopes. The candidate clone, 4F02, was evaluated for neutralising, haemagglutination inhibiting, and HA-mediated fusion-inhibitory, Fc receptor-mediated effector functions in vitro, and for protective efficacy in a lethal mouse challenge model.
Core methods
We screened a panel of previously reported H3-HA-reactive human mAbs to identify mAbs recognising conserved epitopes. Cryo-electron microscopy was used to define the structural basis of 4F02 binding. Human sera were screened for antibodies targeting similar epitopes.
Main results

Interpretation and novelty
Characterisation of the 4F02 epitope reveals a previously underappreciated site of vulnerability at the H3-HA head-stem interface. This finding expands our understanding of conserved epitopes and provides a target for the development of influenza vaccines resilient to antigenic change.
Scientific and public-health significance
This finding provides a potential target for the development of influenza vaccines that are resilient to antigenic change, which could improve public health preparedness against seasonal influenza outbreaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on in vitro and mouse model data, which may not fully represent human immune responses. The evidence is strong as it directly identifies and characterises a conserved epitope on H3-HA with functional validation.

IFITM3功能图谱解析其抗病毒药物核心并阐明人类流感易感性

Functional mapping of IFITM3 resolves its antiviral core and deciphers human influenza susceptibility
作者： Jian Chen, Shufa Zheng, Jinjin Qi, Haibo Wu, Hangping Yao, Min Zheng
摘要中文翻译干扰素诱导的跨膜蛋白 3 (IFITM3) 的遗传变异导致流感结果的个体间差异,但 IFITM3 变异的全面功能图谱仍然无法获得。在这里,我们使用深度突变扫描展示了三种甲型流感毒株的 2,898 个 IFITM3 变体的高分辨率功能图谱。我们的数据揭示了功能受限的中心区域,提供了与 II 型膜拓扑一致的功能证据,并突出了与 抗病毒药物 活性相关的结构特征。与 AlphaMissense 的比较揭示了预测非结构化 N 末端基序和脂质嵌入域内变体影响的系统局限性。至关重要的是,计算模型无法识别 抗病毒药物 活性增强的变体,例如 G133Q。通过将该图谱与 ClinVar 和 gnomAD 集成,我们提供了可能影响感染易感性的罕见人类等位基因的功能读数。这些发现为解释人类变异提供了资源,并建立了研究 宿主 导向的 抗病毒药物 机制的框架。这项研究使用深度突变扫描生成了包含 2,898 个 IFITM3 变体的高分辨率功能图谱。所得图谱解析了该蛋白质的膜内 抗病毒药物 核心,并确定了决定流感感染易感性的关键罕见人类等位基因

Functional mapping of IFITM3 resolves its antiviral core and deciphers human influenza susceptibility

Authors: Jian Chen, Shufa Zheng, Jinjin Qi, Haibo Wu, Hangping Yao, Min Zheng
Original abstract (English)
Genetic variation in interferon-induced transmembrane protein 3 (IFITM3) contributes to inter-individual differences in influenza outcome, yet a comprehensive functional map of IFITM3 variation remains unavailable. Here we show a high-resolution functional atlas of 2,898 IFITM3 variants across three influenza A strains using deep mutational scanning. Our data reveals a functionally constrained central region, providing functional evidence consistent with a Type II membrane topology and highlighting structural features associated with antiviral activity. Comparison with AlphaMissense reveals systematic limitations in predicting the effects of variants within unstructured N-terminal motifs and lipid-embedded domains. Crucially, computational models fail to recognize variants with enhanced antiviral activity, such as G133Q. By integrating this atlas with ClinVar and gnomAD, we provide a functional readout for rare human alleles that may influence susceptibility to infection. These findings provide a resource for interpreting human variation and establish a framework for studying host-directed antiviral mechanisms. This study generates a high-resolution functional atlas of 2,898 IFITM3 variants using deep mutational scanning. The resulting map resolves the protein’s intramembrane antiviral core and identifies key rare human alleles that dictate susceptibility to influenza infection

甲型流感病毒的 宿主 内抗原选择在随机性上占主导地位,但受到 免疫应答 的适应性权衡和时间安排的限制

Within-host antigenic selection of influenza A virus dominates over stochasticity but is limited by fitness tradeoffs and timing of the immune response
作者： Vedhika Raghunathan, Christina Marcelo Leyson, Matthew A. Gaddy, Lucía Ortiz, Nahara Vargas-Maldonado, Jens Wrammert, Georgii A. Bazykin, Daniel B. Weissman, David VanInsberghe, Anice C. Lowen
摘要中文翻译摘要 尽管在全球范围内存在抗原漂移,甲型H3N2流感病毒的抗原变异体在预免疫宿主群体中并未被轻易观察到。本研究测试了适合度权衡、免疫压力时机以及随机效应在预免疫宿主群体内阻碍抗原选择的程度。我们使用了遗传条形码标记的甲型H3N2流感病毒A/德克萨斯/50/2012株(Tx/12)在豚鼠模型中探索这些动态。抗原变异体的正向选择依赖于感染早期施加的强免疫压力。然而,当抗原变化的适合度权衡减弱时,较低强度且较晚施加的免疫压力有利于抗原变异体。在所有条件下,条形码动态显示出中等程度的随机效应。研究结果表明,随机效应并未阻碍急性流感病毒感染期间的抗原选择。抗原逃逸现象罕见的原因可能更多源于低突变供应、适合度权衡以及感染与抗体回忆之间的固有延迟。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在探讨甲型H3N2流感病毒在预免疫宿主群体内抗原选择的作用,并试图解答为何尽管全球范围内存在抗原漂移,抗原变异体仍不常被观察到这一问题。
设计与对象
本研究采用豚鼠模型,使用遗传条形码标记的甲型H3N2流感病毒A/德克萨斯/50/2012株,以测试适合度权衡、免疫压力时机及随机效应如何影响急性感染期间的抗原选择。
核心方法
研究人员在豚鼠模型中使用遗传条形码标记的甲型H3N2流感病毒A/德克萨斯/50/2012株,以研究抗原选择的动态,包括免疫压力时机和适合度权衡的影响。
主要结果
条形码动态在所有实验条件下均显示出中等程度的随机效应,但仅在免疫压力强且感染早期施加时,抗原变异体才被观察到正向选择。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,随机效应并未阻止急性流感病毒感染期间的抗原选择,而免疫压力时机和适合度权衡是决定抗原变异体成功与否的关键因素。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果为理解预免疫宿主群体内抗原逃逸的机制提供了见解,有助于指导疫苗设计策略及病毒在人群中抗原漂移的研究。
局限与证据强度
本研究局限于豚鼠模型,未考虑人类特异性因素,但由于使用了条形码病毒及受控实验条件,证据强度较高。

Within-host antigenic selection of influenza A virus dominates over stochasticity but is limited by fitness tradeoffs and timing of the immune response

Authors: Vedhika Raghunathan, Christina Marcelo Leyson, Matthew A. Gaddy, Lucía Ortiz, Nahara Vargas-Maldonado, Jens Wrammert, Georgii A. Bazykin, Daniel B. Weissman, David VanInsberghe, Anice C. Lowen
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Despite antigenic evolution at the global scale, positive selection of influenza virus antigenic variants is not readily observed within hosts. Here, we tested the extent to which fitness tradeoffs, the timing of immune pressure, and stochastic effects impede antigenic selection within pre-immune hosts. We used genetically barcoded influenza A/Texas/50/2012 (H3N2) viruses (Tx/12) in a guinea pig model to probe these dynamics. Positive selection of an antigenic variant was reliant on a high strength of immune pressure acting early in infection. However, when fitness tradeoffs of the antigenic change were lessened, a lower strength and later introduction of immune pressure favored the antigenic variant. In all conditions, barcode dynamics revealed moderate stochastic effects. Our results suggest that stochastic evolution does not impede selection during acute influenza virus infection. The rarity of antigenic escape may instead stem from low mutational supply, fitness tradeoffs, and the intrinsic delay between infection and antibody recall.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the role of antigenic selection in influenza virus evolution within hosts, addressing the question of why antigenic variants are not commonly observed despite global antigenic evolution.
Design and population
The study used a guinea pig model with genetically barcoded influenza A/Texas/50/2012 (H3N2) viruses to test how fitness tradeoffs, immune timing, and stochastic effects influence antigenic selection during acute infection.
Core methods
The researchers employed genetically barcoded influenza A/Texas/50/2012 (H3N2) viruses in a guinea pig model to examine the dynamics of antigenic selection, including the impact of immune pressure timing and fitness tradeoffs.
Main results
Barcode dynamics showed moderate stochastic effects across all conditions, but positive selection of an antigenic variant was observed only when immune pressure was strong and early in infection.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that stochastic evolution does not prevent antigenic selection during acute influenza virus infection, and that immune pressure timing and fitness tradeoffs are key factors in determining the success of antigenic variants.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results provide insights into the mechanisms of antigenic escape within hosts, which could inform strategies for vaccine design and understanding viral evolution in seasonal influenza outbreaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a guinea pig model and does not account for human-specific factors, but the evidence is strong due to the use of barcoded viruses and controlled experimental conditions.

基于适配子的蛋白酶体靶向嵌合体(PROTAC)靶向降解甲型流感病毒核蛋白用于抗病毒药物治疗

Targeted degradation of Influenza a virus nucleoprotein via aptamer-based PROTACs for antiviral therapy
作者： Weiqiang Li, Yong Ju, Yaoyao Gao, Jinkun Peng, Nan Qi, Li W, Ju Y, Gao Y, Peng J, Qi N.
摘要中文翻译甲型流感病毒(IAV)因其高突变性和快速传播能力对公共卫生构成严重威胁。病毒核蛋白(NP)作为高度保守且必不可少的组分,已成为抗病毒药物治疗的理想靶标。然而,其生物学功能难以通过传统药物调控,且迄今为止尚无针对NP的治疗药物上市。本研究报道了一种基于适配子的蛋白酶体靶向嵌合体(PROTAC)用于靶向降解IAV NP。利用Direct-to-Biology(D2B)平台,我们高效筛选并鉴定出NP-PROTAC#4为功能性候选分子。随后,开发了NP-PROTAC#4的脂质纳米颗粒(LNP)制剂(LNP@NP-PROTAC#4)以实现有效的细胞内递送。值得注意的是,LNP@NP-PROTAC#4在体外和体内均展现出显著的抗病毒药物活性。机制研究表明,NP-PROTAC#4通过靶向并经由泛素-蛋白酶体系统降解NP发挥抗病毒药物作用。综上所述,本研究首次证实NP靶向PROTAC降解剂具有治疗效果,为IAV提出了一种新型治疗策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
甲型流感病毒(IAV)因其高突变性和快速传播能力对公共卫生构成严重威胁。病毒核蛋白(NP)作为高度保守且必不可少的组分,已成为抗病毒药物治疗的理想靶标。
设计与对象
本研究开发并应用了一种基于适配子的蛋白酶体靶向嵌合体(PROTAC)用于靶向降解IAV NP。研究采用Direct-to-Biology(D2B)平台筛选并鉴定出NP-PROTAC#4为功能性候选分子。
核心方法
本研究采用NP-PROTAC#4的脂质纳米颗粒(LNP)制剂(LNP@NP-PROTAC#4)实现有效的细胞内递送。方法包括PROTAC分子的开发及其制剂化为LNP,用于靶向降解IAV NP。
主要结果
值得注意的是,LNP@NP-PROTAC#4在体外和体内均展现出显著的抗病毒药物活性。
解释与创新
综上所述,本研究首次证实NP靶向PROTAC降解剂具有治疗效果,为IAV提出了一种新型治疗策略。
科研与公卫意义
我们的研究结果提出了一种治疗 IAV 感染 治疗 的有前景的新方法,有可能提供更有效、更有针对性的 抗病毒药物 治疗。
局限与证据强度
本研究未报告局限性,但证据基于体外和体内实验,可能无法完全代表临床结果。

Targeted degradation of Influenza a virus nucleoprotein via aptamer-based PROTACs for antiviral therapy

Authors: Weiqiang Li, Yong Ju, Yaoyao Gao, Jinkun Peng, Nan Qi, Li W, Ju Y, Gao Y, Peng J, Qi N.
Original abstract (English)
Influenza A virus (IAV) poses a serious threat to public health due to its high mutability and rapid transmissibility. The viral nucleoprotein (NP), a highly conserved and essential component, has emerged as an ideal target for antiviral therapies. However, its biological function has proven challenging to modulate with conventional drugs, and no NP-targeting therapeutics have reached the market so far. Here, we report the development and application of an aptamer-based proteolysis-targeting chimera (PROTAC) for the targeted degradation of IAV NP. Utilizing the Direct-to-Biology (D2B) platform, we efficiently screened and identified NP-PROTAC#4 as a functional candidate. Subsequently, we developed a lipid nanoparticle (LNP) formulation of NP‐PROTAC#4 (LNP@NP‐PROTAC#4) for effective intracellular delivery. Importantly, LNP@NP-PROTAC#4 demonstrated potent antiviral activity both in vitro and in vivo. Mechanistically, NP-PROTAC#4 exerts its antiviral effects by targeting and degrading NP via the ubiquitin-proteasome system. In conclusion, our findings provide the first evidence that NP-targeted PROTAC degrader exhibits therapeutic effects, proposing a novel therapeutic strategy for IAV.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Influenza A virus (IAV) poses a serious threat to public health due to its high mutability and rapid transmissibility. The viral nucleoprotein (NP), a highly conserved and essential component, has emerged as an ideal target for antiviral therapies.
Design and population
The study involved the development and application of an aptamer-based proteolysis-targeting chimera (PROTAC) for the targeted degradation of IAV NP. The research was conducted using the Direct-to-Biology (D2B) platform to screen and identify NP-PROTAC#4 as a functional candidate.
Core methods
The study utilized a lipid nanoparticle (LNP) formulation of NP-PROTAC#4 (LNP@NP-PROTAC#4) for effective intracellular delivery. The methods included the development of the PROTAC molecule and its formulation into LNPs for targeted degradation of IAV NP.
Main results
Importantly, LNP@NP-PROTAC#4 demonstrated potent antiviral activity both in vitro and in vivo.
Interpretation and novelty
In conclusion, our findings provide the first evidence that NP-targeted PROTAC degrader exhibits therapeutic effects, proposing a novel therapeutic strategy for IAV.
Scientific and public-health significance
Our findings suggest a promising new approach for the treatment of IAV infections, potentially offering a more effective and targeted antiviral therapy.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study does not report limitations, but the evidence is based on in vitro and in vivo experiments, which may not fully represent clinical outcomes.

流感A病毒保守表位候选区域及ESM-2序列特征基准

Conserved influenza A epitope candidate regions and a benchmark of ESM-2 sequence features
作者： Qingxiu Li, Zhenjun Li
摘要中文翻译甲型流感病毒 抗原漂移 迫使每年重新配制 疫苗,从而激发了对可支持广泛保护性 疫苗 的保守表位候选者的搜索。我们系统地筛选了甲型流感病毒序列(H1N1、H3N2、H5N1;九种病毒蛋白),以确定 98 个保守候选区域,其中 38 个在 H1N1、H3N2 和 H5N1 共有序列中是相同的,全部位于聚合酶复合物和核蛋白(PB2、PB1、PA、NP)中,而 10 个表面糖蛋白(HA/NA)候选区域是亚型特异性的。然后,我们对两个蛋白质语言模型 (ESM-2) 特征与对齐保守性进行了基准测试。组屏蔽对数概率与 MSA 保守性适度相关(HA 的 Spearman rho = 0.25 至 0.39),但没有为 T 细胞表位辨别提供增量值(AUROC 的变化 +0.004,p = 0.46);注意力衍生的接触密度并不是有效的溶剂可及性代理。精心设计的抗体表位基准(22 个簇,5 个支持中和)对于高置信度 B 细胞测试来说动力不足。我们记录数据质量和可重复性缺陷(长度异质性、坐标映射和伪复制)并发布可审核的基准。这些结果提供了可审核的候选资源,并表明,在评估的基准中,ESM-2 序列评分并没有改善除比对衍生的保护之外的表位优先顺序。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
流感A病毒每年需进行疫苗株更新(如甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09、甲型H3N2流感病毒等),这推动了对可支持广泛保护性疫苗的保守表位候选区域的搜索。
设计与对象
我们系统地筛选了甲型流感病毒序列(H1N1、H3N2、H5N1;九种病毒蛋白),以确定 98 个保守候选区域,其中 38 个在 H1N1、H3N2 和 H5N1 共有序列中是相同的,全部位于聚合酶复合物和核蛋白(PB2、PB1、PA、NP)中,而 10 个表面糖蛋白(HA/NA)候选区域是亚型特异性的。
核心方法
随后,我们将两种蛋白质语言模型(ESM-2)特征与多序列比对保守性进行基准测试。
主要结果
组掩码对数似然概率与血凝素(HA)的多序列比对保守性呈中等相关性(Spearman rho值为0.25至0.39),但未为T细胞表位鉴别提供增量价值(AUROC变化+0.004,p=0.46);注意力衍生的接触密度并非有效的溶剂可及性代理。
解释与创新
用于抗体表位的精选基准(22个簇,其中5个中和作用支持)在高置信度B细胞检测中缺乏统计功效。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果提供了可审计的候选资源,并表明在所评估的基准中,ESM-2序列评分未能在表位优先级排序方面超越基于比对的保守性。
局限与证据强度
我们记录了数据质量与可重复性问题(序列长度异质性、坐标映射及伪重复),并发布了可审计的基准。

Conserved influenza A epitope candidate regions and a benchmark of ESM-2 sequence features

Authors: Qingxiu Li, Zhenjun Li
Original abstract (English)
Influenza A virus antigenic drift forces annual vaccine reformulation, motivating the search for conserved epitope candidates that could support broadly protective vaccines. We systematically screened influenza A virus sequences (H1N1, H3N2, H5N1; nine viral proteins) to define 98 conserved candidate regions, 38 of which were identical across the H1N1, H3N2, and H5N1 consensus sequences, all in the polymerase complex and nucleoprotein (PB2, PB1, PA, NP), whereas the ten surface-glycoprotein (HA/NA) candidates were subtype-specific. We then benchmarked two protein-language-model (ESM-2) features against alignment conservation. Group-masked log-probability correlated moderately with MSA conservation (Spearman rho = 0.25 to 0.39 for HA) but provided no incremental value for T-cell epitope discrimination (change in AUROC +0.004, p = 0.46); attention-derived contact-density was not a valid solvent-accessibility proxy. A curated antibody-epitope benchmark (22 clusters, 5 neutralization-supported) was underpowered for a high-confidence B-cell test. We document data-quality and reproducibility pitfalls (length heterogeneity, coordinate mapping, and pseudoreplication) and release the auditable benchmark. These results provide an auditable candidate resource and show that, in the evaluated benchmarks, ESM-2 sequence scores did not improve epitope prioritization beyond alignment-derived conservation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Influenza A virus antigenic drift forces annual vaccine reformulation, motivating the search for conserved epitope candidates that could support broadly protective vaccines.
Design and population
We systematically screened influenza A virus sequences (H1N1, H3N2, H5N1; nine viral proteins) to define 98 conserved candidate regions, 38 of which were identical across the H1N1, H3N2, and H5N1 consensus sequences, all in the polymerase complex and nucleoprotein (PB2, PB1, PA, NP), whereas the ten surface-glycoprotein (HA/NA) candidates were subtype-specific.
Core methods
We then benchmarked two protein-language-model (ESM-2) features against alignment conservation.
Main results
Group-masked log-probability correlated moderately with MSA conservation (Spearman rho = 0.25 to 0.39 for HA) but provided no incremental value for T-cell epitope discrimination (change in AUROC +0.004, p = 0.46); attention-derived contact-density was not a valid solvent-accessibility proxy.
Interpretation and novelty
A curated antibody-epitope benchmark (22 clusters, 5 neutralization-supported) was underpowered for a high-confidence B-cell test.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results provide an auditable candidate resource and show that, in the evaluated benchmarks, ESM-2 sequence scores did not improve epitope prioritization beyond alignment-derived conservation.
Limitations and evidence strength
We document data-quality and reproducibility pitfalls (length heterogeneity, coordinate mapping, and pseudoreplication) and release the auditable benchmark.

一名儿童感染甲型H3N2流感病毒与社区获得性耐甲氧西林敏感金黄色葡萄球菌重症合并感染并出现脓毒性休克、急性呼吸窘迫综合征及坏死性肺炎:罕见病例报告病例报告

Severe co-infection with influenza A virus H3N2 and community-acquired methicillin-susceptible Staphylococcus aureus in a child presenting with septic shock, acute respiratory distress syndrome, and necrotizing pneumonia: a rare case report
作者： Zhangyan Guo, Haifeng Qi, Qian Zhang, Yi Wang, Yanqiang Du
摘要中文翻译背景 流感合并金黄色葡萄球菌(S. aureus)感染可导致儿童出现迅速致命的坏死性肺炎、脓毒性休克及急性呼吸窘迫综合征(ARDS)。尽管耐甲氧西林金黄色葡萄球菌(MRSA)常被重点关注,但社区获得性耐甲氧西林敏感金黄色葡萄球菌(CA-MSSA)也可引发同样严重的疾病。病例报告 本文报告一名8岁4个月男童感染甲型H3N2流感病毒后出现脓毒性休克及难治性低氧血症,需立即行气管插管。尽管采用最大通气治疗,但持续呼吸衰竭,于第1天启动静脉-静脉体外膜肺氧合(VV-ECMO)。宏基因组下一代测序鉴定出金黄色葡萄球菌为优势病原体,支气管肺泡灌洗液培养后确认为MSSA。万古霉素治疗无效后,改用利奈唑胺。然而,在第15天利奈唑胺治疗期间,患者出现严重利奈唑胺诱导性乳酸性酸中毒(LILA),停药3天后症状缓解。临床过程进一步并发气胸及多重耐药菌继发感染。经过54天重症监护,患者康复出院。结论 本病例强调,在流感季节,对于儿童快速进展性肺炎及休克患者,即使疑似CA-MSSA感染,也应考虑早期经验性抗葡萄球菌治疗。此外,利奈唑胺治疗期间需常规监测血乳酸水平,以便及时识别并处理危及生命的LILA。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在强调儿童甲型流感病毒 H3N2 和社区获得性甲氧西林敏感金黄色葡萄球菌混合感染的潜在严重性,因为此类混合感染可导致迅速致命的坏死性肺炎、感染性休克和急性呼吸窘迫综合征 (ARDS)。
设计与对象
本研究为一罕见病例报告,描述一名8岁4个月男童因感染甲型H3N2流感病毒与社区获得性耐甲氧西林敏感金黄色葡萄球菌重症合并感染,出现脓毒性休克及难治性低氧血症。患者需立即行气管插管,并接受利奈唑胺治疗,后因严重利奈唑胺诱导性乳酸性酸中毒而中断用药。
核心方法
使用宏基因组下一代测序来鉴定主要病原体,支气管肺泡灌洗液培养证实存在甲氧西林敏感金黄色葡萄球菌。患者的临床病程通过抗葡萄球菌治疗进行管理,并监测乳酸性酸中毒等并发症。
主要结果
患者出现脓毒性休克及难治性低氧血症,需行气管插管并在第1天启动静脉-静脉体外膜肺氧合(VV-ECMO)。尽管接受利奈唑胺治疗,患者仍出现严重利奈唑胺诱导性乳酸性酸中毒,停药后症状缓解。患者最终于54天后康复出院。
解释与创新
作者认为,在流感季节,对于儿童快速进展性肺炎及休克患者,即使疑似社区获得性耐甲氧西林敏感金黄色葡萄球菌感染,也应考虑早期经验性抗葡萄球菌治疗。本病例强调识别并处理利奈唑胺治疗相关严重并发症的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果提示,在流感季节,需警惕诊断并治疗儿童甲型H3N2流感病毒与社区获得性耐甲氧西林敏感金黄色葡萄球菌合并感染。强调利奈唑胺治疗期间常规监测血乳酸水平,以便及时发现并处理危及生命的乳酸性酸中毒。
局限与证据强度
作为一项罕见的 病例报告,该研究缺乏大型 队列研究 研究的统计功效和普遍性。然而,所描述的具体病例的证据是强有力的,并且研​​究结果与管理严重流感相关合并感染的临床实践相关。

Severe co-infection with influenza A virus H3N2 and community-acquired methicillin-susceptible Staphylococcus aureus in a child presenting with septic shock, acute respiratory distress syndrome, and necrotizing pneumonia: a rare case report

Authors: Zhangyan Guo, Haifeng Qi, Qian Zhang, Yi Wang, Yanqiang Du
Original abstract (English)
Background Influenza co-infection with Staphylococcus aureus ( S. aureus ) can cause rapidly fatal necrotizing pneumonia, septic shock, and acute respiratory distress syndrome (ARDS) in children. Although methicillin-resistant S. aureus is often highlighted, community-acquired methicillin-susceptible S. aureus (CA-MSSA) can also produce equally severe disease. Case presentation We report an 8-year-4-month-old male with influenza A (H3N2) who developed septic shock and refractory hypoxemia, requiring immediate intubation. Due to persisting respiratory failure despite maximal ventilation, veno-venous extracorporeal membrane oxygenation (VV-ECMO) was initiated on day 1. Metagenomic next-generation sequencing identified S. aureus as the dominant pathogen, and bronchoalveolar lavage fluid culture later confirmed MSSA. After vancomycin failed clinically, the regimen was switched to linezolid. However, on day 15 of linezolid therapy, the patient developed severe linezolid-induced lactic acidosis (LILA), which resolved within 3 days of stopping the drug. The clinical course was further complicated by pneumothorax and multidrug-resistant organism superinfections. After 54 days of intensive care, the patient was discharged in good condition. Conclusion This case underscores that during influenza seasons, early empirical anti-staphylococcal therapy should be considered in children with rapidly progressive pneumonia and shock, even when CA-MSSA is suspected. Additionally, routine lactate monitoring is critical during linezolid therapy to enable prompt recognition and management of life-threatening LILA.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to highlight the potential severity of co-infection with influenza A virus H3N2 and community-acquired methicillin-susceptible Staphylococcus aureus in children, as such co-infections can lead to rapidly fatal necrotizing pneumonia, septic shock, and acute respiratory distress syndrome (ARDS).
Design and population
The study is a rare case report describing an 8-year-4-month-old male child who developed septic shock and refractory hypoxemia due to co-infection with influenza A (H3N2) and community-acquired methicillin-susceptible Staphylococcus aureus. The patient required immediate intubation and was treated with linezolid, which later led to severe linezolid-induced lactic acidosis.
Core methods
Metagenomic next-generation sequencing was used to identify the dominant pathogen, and bronchoalveolar lavage fluid culture confirmed the presence of methicillin-susceptible Staphylococcus aureus. The patient's clinical course was managed with anti-staphylococcal therapy and monitored for complications such as lactic acidosis.
Main results
The patient developed septic shock and refractory hypoxemia, necessitating intubation and veno-venous extracorporeal membrane oxygenation (VV-ECMO) on day 1. Despite treatment with linezolid, the patient experienced severe linezolid-induced lactic acidosis, which resolved after discontinuation of the drug. The patient was eventually discharged in good condition after 54 days of intensive care.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that during influenza seasons, early empirical anti-staphylococcal therapy should be considered in children with rapidly progressive pneumonia and shock, even when community-acquired methicillin-susceptible Staphylococcus aureus is suspected. This case highlights the importance of recognizing and managing severe complications associated with linezolid therapy.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the need for vigilance in diagnosing and treating co-infections involving influenza A (H3N2) and community-acquired methicillin-susceptible Staphylococcus aureus in children, particularly during influenza seasons. Routine lactate monitoring during linezolid therapy is emphasized to detect and manage life-threatening lactic acidosis.
Limitations and evidence strength
As a rare case report, the study lacks the statistical power and generalizability of larger cohort studies. However, the evidence is strong for the specific case described, and the findings are relevant to clinical practice in managing severe influenza-related co-infections.

2009年甲型H1N1流感病毒大流行以来越南成人流感甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09相关肺炎患者的ICU收治与呼吸衰竭负担:一项为期10年的回顾性队列研究

ICU admission and mortality in adult patients with influenza A(H1N1)pdm09-related pneumonia in Vietnam since the 2009 H1N1 pandemic: A 10-year cohort study
作者： Quang Minh Ho, Bich Thuy Duong, Le Nhu Tung Nguyen, Tri Nugraha Susilawati, Ngoc Minh Tam Bui, Thanh Truc Thai, David J. Muscatello, Anthony Sunjaya, Siyu Chen, Thanh Nguyen Nguyen
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估2009年甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09大流行以来越南成人甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09相关肺炎患者的ICU收治与呼吸衰竭负担,因甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09仍是全球重症流感的重要病因。
设计与对象
本研究为一项在越南传染病领先医院开展的为期10年回顾性队列研究,纳入2009年至2019年间因实验室确诊甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09感染住院的成人患者。
核心方法
从实验室确诊的 甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09 感染患者的医疗记录中回顾性收集人口统计学、临床、实验室、治疗 和结果数据。
主要结果
Among 729 patients with laboratory-confirmed A(H1N1)pdm09 infection, 21.7% (158/729) developed pneumonia. Among those with pneumonia, 36.7% developed moderate-to-severe acute respiratory distress syndrome, 15.2% required invasive ventilation, 48.7% were admitted to the ICU, and 8.2% died.
解释与创新
作者解读认为甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09相关肺炎所致呼吸衰竭负担仍然严重,强调需密切监测高风险人群。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果提示需针对性开展监测与干预,重点关注年龄>60岁、存在合并症及实验室异常的患者,以预防病情恶化需行ICU治疗。
局限与证据强度
本研究受限于回顾性设计与潜在缺失数据风险,但因样本量大且对预测因子进行了详细分析,证据强度较高。

ICU admission and mortality in adult patients with influenza A(H1N1)pdm09-related pneumonia in Vietnam since the 2009 H1N1 pandemic: A 10-year cohort study

Authors: Quang Minh Ho, Bich Thuy Duong, Le Nhu Tung Nguyen, Tri Nugraha Susilawati, Ngoc Minh Tam Bui, Thanh Truc Thai, David J. Muscatello, Anthony Sunjaya, Siyu Chen, Thanh Nguyen Nguyen
Original abstract (English)
Background The A(H1N1)pdm09 virus remains an important cause of severe influenza worldwide. This study examined the burden of ICU admission, mortality, and associated predictors among adults with A(H1N1)pdm09-related pneumonia treated at a leading infectious diseases hospital in Vietnam. Methods Demographic, clinical, laboratory, treatment, and outcome data were retrospectively collected from the medical records of adults hospitalized with laboratory-confirmed A(H1N1)pdm09 infection between 2009 and 2019. Results Among 729 patients with laboratory-confirmed A(H1N1)pdm09 infection, 21.7% (158/729) developed pneumonia. Among patients with pneumonia, 36.7% developed moderate-to-severe acute respiratory distress syndrome, 15.2% required invasive ventilation, 48.7% were admitted to the ICU, and 8.2% died. Independent predictors of ICU admission included age older than 60 years (AOR 18.91, 95% CI 3.60–99.24), the presence of comorbidities (AOR 9.39, 95% CI 2.75–32.06), and laboratory evidence of systemic inflammation, renal dysfunction, and hepatic involvement. Intensified antiviral therapy (AOR = 8.577, 95%CI 1.416–83.341, P = 0.020), and invasive ventilation (AOR 8.426, 95%CI 1.105–141.366, P = 0.039) were independently associated with mortality. Conclusions Mortality associated with A(H1N1)pdm09-related pneumonia remains substantial. Older patients, those with comorbidities, and those presenting with abnormal laboratory findings should be monitored closely for deterioration requiring intensive care. The study did not demonstrate improved outcomes with intensified antiviral regimens. Prospective studies are required before definitive treatment recommendations can be made.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to assess the burden of ICU admission and mortality among adults with A(H1N1)pdm09-related pneumonia in Vietnam since the 2009 pandemic, as the A(H1N1)pdm09 virus remains an important cause of severe influenza worldwide.
Design and population
This was a 10-year cohort study conducted at a leading infectious diseases hospital in Vietnam, examining adults hospitalized with laboratory-confirmed A(H1N1)pdm09 infection between 2009 and 2019.
Core methods
Demographic, clinical, laboratory, treatment, and outcome data were retrospectively collected from medical records of patients with laboratory-confirmed A(H1N1)pdm09 infection.
Main results
Among 729 patients with laboratory-confirmed A(H1N1)pdm09 infection, 21.7% (158/729) developed pneumonia. Among those with pneumonia, 36.7% developed moderate-to-severe acute respiratory distress syndrome, 15.2% required invasive ventilation, 48.7% were admitted to the ICU, and 8.2% died.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that mortality associated with A(H1N1)pdm09-related pneumonia remains substantial, highlighting the need for close monitoring of high-risk groups.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the need for targeted surveillance and monitoring of older patients, those with comorbidities, and those with abnormal laboratory findings to prevent deterioration requiring intensive care.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and potential for missing data, but the evidence is strong due to the large sample size and detailed analysis of predictors.

鼻内给药含c-di-AMP佐剂的COBRA基蛋白疫苗可增强对季节性流感病毒的广泛保护作用

Intranasal delivery of a COBRA-based protein vaccine adjuvanted with c-di-AMP enhances breadth of protective immune responses against seasonal influenza viruses
作者： Camila C. S. Caetano, David A. Prots, Matthew H. Thomas, Dylan R. Micallef, Robert A. Richardson, Ted M. Ross
摘要中文翻译季节性流感 疫苗 针对抗原漂移的甲型流感(H1N1 和 H3N2)和乙型流感病毒提供可变保护。使用计算优化的广泛反应性抗原(COBRA)的广泛保护方法可以增强针对不同菌株的免疫力。双-(3',5')-环状二聚腺苷单磷酸 (c-di-AMP) 是一种粘膜佐剂,可增强 疫苗 诱导的 免疫应答。在这里,我们假设鼻内多价 COBRA 血凝素 (HA) 和神经氨酸酶 (NA) 疫苗接种 与 c-di-AMP 会诱导小鼠广泛的体液和细胞免疫。用含有 COBRA 重组 HA 和 NA 或相应野生型抗原的五价 疫苗 制剂对免疫学上未接触过的和免疫前的 DBA/2J 小鼠进行鼻内免疫。制剂以低或高抗原剂量施用,有或没有粘膜佐剂c-di-AMP。评估了体液和细胞 免疫应答,以及甲型流感(H1N1 和 H3N2)和乙型流感病毒攻击后的保护作用。五价 COBRA HA/NA 鼻内 疫苗接种 与 c-di-AMP 佐剂在小鼠模型中诱导强体液和细胞 免疫应答。疫苗接种 在免疫前小鼠中诱导出强劲的、剂量依赖性的总 IgG 和血凝抑制 (HAI) 滴度,以及高频率的抗原特异性抗体分泌细胞和干扰素 γ (IFN-γ) 产生细胞。在较高的 疫苗 剂量下,首次实验的小鼠也主要针对 H1N1 病毒而获得血清保护。在遭受 H1N1、H3N2 和 B 型流感病毒的致命攻击后,接种疫苗的小鼠表现出体重减轻减轻、肺部病毒滴度降低和存活率提高,在幼稚模型和免疫前模型中,在佐剂组中观察到最强的保护作用。这些发现凸显了将具有广泛保护性的 COBRA 疫苗 候选药物与先进的粘膜佐剂相结合,以增强针对抗原漂移流感病毒株的功效的价值。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
季节性流感 疫苗对抗原漂移的甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09、甲型H3N2流感病毒和乙型流感病毒的保护效果存在差异,亟需开发广泛保护性的疫苗策略。本研究旨在验证鼻内多价COBRA血凝素(HA)和神经氨酸酶(NA)疫苗接种(含c-di-AMP佐剂)是否能在小鼠体内诱导广泛的体液和细胞免疫应答。
设计与对象
本研究使用免疫原性未知及预免疫的DBA/2J小鼠,通过鼻内接种含COBRA重组HA和NA或野生型抗原的五价疫苗制剂。制剂以低剂量或高剂量抗原给药,并添加或不添加黏膜佐剂c-di-AMP。
核心方法
评估了体液和细胞免疫应答,以及甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09、甲型H3N2流感病毒和乙型流感病毒攻击后的保护效果。研究测定了总IgG和血凝抑制(HAI)滴度、抗原特异性抗体分泌细胞以及干扰素-γ(IFN-γ)分泌细胞。
主要结果
在致死剂量攻击甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09、甲型H3N2流感病毒和乙型流感病毒后,接种疫苗的小鼠表现出体重减轻减少、肺部病毒滴度降低和存活率提高。在含佐剂组中观察到最强的保护效果,且在免疫原性未知及预免疫模型中均成立。
解释与创新
作者解释说,将具有广泛保护性的 COBRA 疫苗 候选药物与粘膜佐剂 c-di-AMP 相结合,可增强针对抗原漂移流感病毒株的功效。他们强调了这种方法对于诱导广泛的体液和细胞免疫的价值。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,鼻内给药含c-di-AMP佐剂的COBRA基蛋白疫苗接种有望为开发针对季节性流感株提供更广泛保护的下一代流感疫苗提供依据。该方法或可解决现有疫苗在抗原漂移情境下的局限性。
局限与证据强度
本研究在小鼠模型中进行,限制了结果向人类的直接外推。尽管证据在小鼠中显示出强效免疫原性和保护作用,仍需进一步在人体临床试验中验证这些发现,并评估其长期有效性和安全性。

Intranasal delivery of a COBRA-based protein vaccine adjuvanted with c-di-AMP enhances breadth of protective immune responses against seasonal influenza viruses

Authors: Camila C. S. Caetano, David A. Prots, Matthew H. Thomas, Dylan R. Micallef, Robert A. Richardson, Ted M. Ross
Original abstract (English)
Seasonal influenza vaccines provide variable protection against antigenically drifted influenza A (H1N1 and H3N2) and influenza B viruses. Broadly protective approaches using computationally optimized broadly reactive antigens (COBRAs) can enhance immunity against diverse strains. Bis-(3′,5′)-cyclic dimeric adenosine monophosphate (c-di-AMP) is a mucosal adjuvant that boosts vaccine-induced immune responses. Here, we hypothesized that intranasal multivalent COBRA hemagglutinin (HA) and neuraminidase (NA) vaccination with c-di-AMP would induce broad humoral and cellular immunity in mice. Immunologically naïve and pre-immune DBA/2J mice were intranasally immunized with pentavalent vaccine formulations containing COBRA recombinant HA and NA or corresponding wild-type antigens. Formulations were administered at low or high antigen doses, with or without the mucosal adjuvant c-di-AMP. Humoral and cellular immune responses were evaluated, along with protection following influenza A (H1N1 and H3N2) and influenza B viral challenge. Pentavalent COBRA HA/NA intranasal vaccination adjuvanted with c-di-AMP induced strong humoral and cellular immune responses in murine models. Vaccination induced robust, dose-dependent total IgG and hemagglutination inhibition (HAI) titers, along with high frequencies of antigen-specific antibody-secreting cells and interferon-γ (IFN-γ)-producing cells in pre-immune mice. Naïve mice were also seroprotected mainly against H1N1 at higher vaccine doses. Following lethal challenge with H1N1, H3N2, and influenza B viruses, vaccinated mice exhibited reduced weight loss, decreased lung viral titers, and enhanced survival, with the strongest protection observed in adjuvanted groups across both naïve and pre-immune models. These findings highlight the value of combining broadly protective COBRA vaccine candidates with advanced mucosal adjuvants to enhance efficacy against antigenically drifted influenza virus strains.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Seasonal influenza vaccines show variable protection against antigenically drifted influenza A (H1N1 and H3N2) and influenza B viruses, creating a need for broadly protective vaccine strategies. The study aimed to test whether intranasal multivalent COBRA hemagglutinin (HA) and neuraminidase (NA) vaccination adjuvanted with c-di-AMP could induce broad humoral and cellular immunity in mice.
Design and population
The study used immunologically naïve and pre-immune DBA/2J mice intranasally immunized with pentavalent vaccine formulations containing COBRA recombinant HA and NA or wild-type antigens. Formulations were administered at low or high antigen doses, with or without the mucosal adjuvant c-di-AMP.
Core methods
Humoral and cellular immune responses were evaluated, along with protection following influenza A (H1N1 and H3N2) and influenza B viral challenge. The study assessed total IgG and hemagglutination inhibition (HAI) titers, antigen-specific antibody-secreting cells, and interferon-γ (IFN-γ)-producing cells.
Main results
Following lethal challenge with H1N1, H3N2, and influenza B viruses, vaccinated mice exhibited reduced weight loss, decreased lung viral titers, and enhanced survival. The strongest protection was observed in adjuvanted groups across both naïve and pre-immune models.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that combining broadly protective COBRA vaccine candidates with the mucosal adjuvant c-di-AMP enhanced efficacy against antigenically drifted influenza virus strains. They highlighted the value of this approach for inducing broad humoral and cellular immunity.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings suggest that intranasal COBRA-based protein vaccination with c-di-AMP could inform the development of next-generation influenza vaccines with broader protection against seasonal strains. The approach may address limitations of current vaccines in antigenically drifted settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was conducted in murine models, limiting direct translation to humans. While the evidence demonstrates robust immunogenicity and protection in mice, further research is needed to validate these findings in human clinical trials and assess long-term efficacy and safety.

非编码RNA与RIG-I/MAVS信号通路:流感感染靶向治疗的新见解

Non-coding RNAs and RIG-I/MAVS signaling pathway: Insights for targeted therapy in influenza infection
作者： Yin Wu, Mei Lu, Linsong Hou, Juan Yang, Qing Chen, Hong Pan, Yi Zhang, Wu Y, Lu M, Hou L
摘要中文翻译流行病的暴发可能是由于病毒的快速抗原漂移和转移而导致的,这也会影响现有疫苗的有效性。例如,miR-136 可以作为 RIG-I 的内源配体,直接增强其激活。[]此外,miR-483-3p 通过受感染细胞的外泌体转运,并作为信号分子激活附近细胞中的先天 免疫应答。[]此外,miR-200c 通过降低其负调节因子 contactin 1 (CNTN1) 和 contactin 1 (CNTN1) 的活性来提高 MAVS 蛋白的稳定性。泛素特异性肽酶 25 (USP25),展示了一种增强中央信号传导节点的方法。总的来说,这些发现揭示了流感病毒和 宿主 miRNA 之间的双向联系。它们参与 抗病毒药物 免疫是一个有前途且快速发展的研究领域。流感病毒对全球人口的健康构成重大挑战,每年导致数百万重症病例和数十万人死亡。尽管可以使用神经氨酸酶抑制剂等 抗病毒药物 药物,但耐药菌株的增加表明迫切需要具有不同作用机制的新型治疗策略。开发针对病毒复制所必需的 宿主 导向途径的 治疗 方法和 免疫应答 可能是缓解这一问题的关键一步。该策略的核心关键通路是由视黄酸诱导基因 I (RIG-I) 及其接头蛋白线粒体 抗病毒药物 信号蛋白 (MAVS) 介导的先天免疫感应通路。[] 该轴作为细胞内防御的第一道防线,识别病毒 RNA (vRNA) 并通过诱导 I 型触发有效的 抗病毒药物 状态...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究探讨非编码RNA(ncRNAs)在流感感染过程中调控RIG-I/MAVS信号通路的作用,并强调由于现有抗病毒药物的局限性,亟需开发新型治疗策略。
设计与对象
本研究聚焦于RIG-I/MAVS通路及其与非编码RNA在流感感染背景下的相互作用,重点关注人呼吸道上皮细胞中的先天和适应性免疫应答。
核心方法
本研究采用分子和细胞技术,探索包括miRNA、lncRNA和circRNA在内的非编码RNA如何调控RIG-I/MAVS通路,特别关注circVAMP3对病毒蛋白的结合与隔离作用。
主要结果
研究证明circVAMP3可直接结合并隔离流感病毒核蛋白(NP)和非结构蛋白1(NS1),从而抑制流感病毒复制,如补充图1所示。
解释与创新
研究将结果解读为流感病毒与宿主微小RNA(miRNAs)之间的双向互作证据,提示非编码RNA可作为流感感染干预的新型治疗靶标。
科研与公卫意义
本研究为开发以宿主微小RNA为导向的流感治疗策略提供了理论依据,有望通过靶向调控免疫相关非编码RNA来减轻流感影响。
局限与证据强度
本研究仅聚焦分子机制,缺乏直接临床结局数据,且证据基于体外和计算机模拟分析,可能无法完全反映体内环境。

Non-coding RNAs and RIG-I/MAVS signaling pathway: Insights for targeted therapy in influenza infection

Authors: Yin Wu, Mei Lu, Linsong Hou, Juan Yang, Qing Chen, Hong Pan, Yi Zhang, Wu Y, Lu M, Hou L
Original abstract (English)
The outbreaks of pandemics can result from the rapid antigenic drift and shift of the virus, which can also affect the effectiveness of the existing vaccines. For instance, miR-136 can function as an endogenous ligand for RIG-I, directly increasing its activation.[] In addition, miR-483-3p is transported via exosomes from infected cells and functions as a signaling molecule to activate the innate immune response in nearby cells.[] Moreover, miR-200c improves the stability of MAVS protein by reducing the activity of its negative regulators, contactin 1 (CNTN1) and ubiquitin specific peptidase 25 (USP25), demonstrating a method to strengthen the central signaling node. Collectively, these findings reveal a bidirectional link between the influenza virus and host miRNAs. Their involvement in antiviral immunity is a promising and rapidly evolving field of study. Influenza viruses are a significant challenge to the health of the global population, and are responsible for millions of severe cases and hundreds of thousands of deaths each year. Despite the availability of antiviral drugs like neuraminidase inhibitors, the rise in resistant strains is indicative of the urgent need for novel therapeutic strategies with distinct mechanisms of action. Developing treatment approaches that target host-directed pathways essential for viral replication and the immune response could be a pivotal step in mitigating this issue. The key pathway central to this strategy is the innate immune-sensing pathway mediated by retinoic acid-inducible gene I (RIG-I) and its adaptor protein mitochondrial antiviral-signaling protein (MAVS).[] This axis serves as the first line of intracellular defense, recognizing viral RNA (vRNA) and triggering a potent antiviral state through the induction of type I...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study investigates the role of non-coding RNAs (ncRNAs) in modulating the RIG-I/MAVS signaling pathway during influenza infection, highlighting the need for novel therapeutic strategies due to the limitations of current antiviral drugs.
Design and population
The study focuses on the RIG-I/MAVS pathway and its interaction with ncRNAs in the context of influenza infection, with an emphasis on the innate and adaptive immune responses in human respiratory epithelium.
Core methods
The research employs molecular and cellular techniques to explore how ncRNAs, including miRNAs, lncRNAs, and circRNAs, regulate the RIG-I/MAVS pathway, with specific attention to the binding and sequestration of viral proteins by circVAMP3.
Main results
It was demonstrated that circVAMP3 directly binds to and sequesters the viral nucleoprotein (NP) and non-structural protein 1 (NS1) to prevent influenza virus replication, as shown in Supplementary Figure 1.
Interpretation and novelty
The study interprets the findings as evidence of a bidirectional interaction between the influenza virus and host miRNAs, suggesting that ncRNAs could serve as novel therapeutic targets for antiviral intervention.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings offer insights into the development of host-directed antiviral therapies that could potentially mitigate the impact of influenza by targeting ncRNAs involved in immune regulation.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its focus on molecular mechanisms without direct clinical outcomes, and the evidence is based on in vitro and in silico analyses, which may not fully reflect in vivo conditions.

湿式静电沉淀器对气溶胶化甲型流感病毒收集效率的评估

Evaluation of the Collection Efficiency of a Wet-Type Electrostatic Precipitator for Aerosolized Influenza A Virus
作者： Kazuya Nakamura, Takeshi Nagai, Hitoshi Ishiguro, Keiichi Kobayashi, Kazuhisa Nakagawa, Masahiro Okanojo, Akira Nukazuka
摘要中文翻译空气传播病毒是传染病传播的关键驱动因素,凸显公共卫生监测中可靠检测的重要性。由于大气中病毒浓度极低,高效病毒收集采样器至关重要。尽管已有多种方法利用不同采样器评估收集效率,但尚未开发出标准化采样器。我们此前开发了一种湿式静电沉淀器(WT-ESP),并成功从公共环境中收集了严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2。然而,其对气溶胶化病毒的定量收集效率仍不明确。本研究旨在明确WT-ESP的收集效率。我们通过两种不同方法评估收集效率:直接喷雾法(将含病毒气溶胶直接喷入WT-ESP进气口)和间接喷雾法(将气溶胶分散至封闭空间后再用采样器收集)。直接喷雾测试的收集效率为20.1–50.2%,而间接喷雾测试的收集效率为
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在明确湿式静电沉淀器(WT-ESP)对气溶胶化病毒的收集效率,因大气中病毒浓度极低且公共卫生监测亟需可靠采样器。
设计与对象
本研究采用两种方法评估WT-ESP的收集效率:直接喷雾法(将含病毒气溶胶直接喷入WT-ESP进气口)和间接喷雾法(将气溶胶分散至封闭空间后再用采样器收集)。
核心方法
本研究采用直接喷雾法和间接喷雾法评估WT-ESP的收集效率,后者涉及将气溶胶分散至封闭空间后再进行采样。
主要结果
直接喷雾测试的收集效率为20.1-50.2%,而间接喷雾测试的收集效率为32.8-48.5%。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,WT-ESP可有效收集气溶胶化甲型流感病毒,为公共卫生环境中改善病毒监测提供潜在工具。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,WT-ESP有助于提升气溶胶化甲型流感病毒的检测效果,为更精准的公共卫生监测与应对策略提供支持。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于抽象证据,效率值基于受控实验条件,可能无法完全反映现实世界的情况。

Evaluation of the Collection Efficiency of a Wet-Type Electrostatic Precipitator for Aerosolized Influenza A Virus

Authors: Kazuya Nakamura, Takeshi Nagai, Hitoshi Ishiguro, Keiichi Kobayashi, Kazuhisa Nakagawa, Masahiro Okanojo, Akira Nukazuka
Original abstract (English)
Airborne viruses are a key driver of infectious disease transmission, highlighting the importance of reliable detection in public health surveillance. As atmospheric viral concentrations are very low, a sampler with a high viral collection efficiency is essential. Although multiple approaches for evaluating collection efficiency have been applied using various samplers, no standardized sampler has yet been developed. We previously developed a wet-type electrostatic precipitator (WT-ESP) and successfully collected severe acute respiratory syndrome coronavirus 2 from the public environment. However, its efficiency for quantitative collection of airborne viruses remains unclear. This study aimed to clarify the collection efficiency of the WT-ESP. We evaluated collection efficiency via two different approaches: direct spray, where virus-containing aerosols were sprayed directly into the WT-ESP inlet, and indirect spray, where aerosols were dispersed into a closed space and then collected using the sampler. The direct spray tests achieved 20.1–50.2% collection efficiency, whereas the indirect spray test achieved an efficiency
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to clarify the collection efficiency of the wet-type electrostatic precipitator (WT-ESP) for airborne viruses, as atmospheric viral concentrations are very low and a reliable sampler is essential for public health surveillance.
Design and population
The study evaluated the collection efficiency of the WT-ESP using two approaches: direct spray, where virus-containing aerosols were sprayed directly into the WT-ESP inlet, and indirect spray, where aerosols were dispersed into a closed space and then collected using the sampler.
Core methods
The study used direct spray and indirect spray methods to assess the collection efficiency of the WT-ESP, with the latter involving aerosol dispersion into a closed space before sampling.
Main results
The direct spray tests achieved 20.1–50.2% collection efficiency, whereas the indirect spray test achieved an efficiency of 32.8–48.5%.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate that the WT-ESP can effectively collect airborne influenza A virus, providing a potential tool for improving viral surveillance in public health settings.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results suggest that the WT-ESP could enhance the detection of airborne influenza viruses, supporting more accurate public health monitoring and response strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to abstract-only evidence, and the efficiency values are based on controlled experimental conditions, which may not fully reflect real-world scenarios.

流感A病毒感染后小鼠肺部免疫细胞亚群的剂量依赖性改变

Dose-Dependent Alterations in Lung Immune Subpopulations in Influenza a Virus Infection
作者： Tatiana Betáková, Miriam Mladá, Karin Donátová, Jana Jakubíková
摘要中文翻译本研究旨在表征小鼠感染流感A病毒(IAV)后肺部免疫细胞亚群的调节作用,评估感染剂量、病毒适应性及NS1表达的影响。采用包含10种抗体的免疫表型分析面板,通过多参数流式细胞术对免疫细胞亚群进行表面受体表达分析。在致死剂量感染后,肺部中性粒细胞(表达Ly-6G)显著增加,且此变化与NS1表达无关;相比之下,所有病毒的致死剂量感染均降低了CD163+和F4/80+中性粒细胞亚群。致死剂量感染使肺部CD68+巨噬细胞数量增加,同时在感染后第3天减少了CD163+、CD193+和F4/80+巨噬细胞亚群及F4/80+髓样细胞数量;这些减少与NS1表达及感染剂量无关。在致死剂量流感A病毒感染后,NK细胞中IL-23R+和IL-12Rβ2+亚群上调,而CD193+ NK细胞亚群在感染后第3天减少。NKT细胞分析显示,感染后第3天IL-12Rβ2+ NKT细胞亚群扩增。肺部CD4+ T细胞的适应性免疫分析显示,在WSN感染后选择性增加了Th1样细胞(IL-12Rβ2+ CD4+),无论NS1状态或剂量如何,感染流感A病毒后Th2样细胞(CD193+ CD4+)显著减少,仅在致死剂量感染后CD4+NK1.1+细胞扩增。NS1表达与野生型病毒感染的免疫细胞亚群频率相当;NS1表达未改变亚群组成,而感染剂量调节了其丰度。这些发现拓展了对免疫细胞亚群及其在流感病毒发病机制中可能作用的理解。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在表征小鼠感染流感A病毒(IAV)后肺部免疫细胞亚群的调节作用,评估感染剂量、病毒适应性及NS1表达的影响。
设计与对象
采用包含10种抗体的免疫表型分析面板,通过多参数流式细胞术对免疫细胞亚群进行表面受体表达分析。
核心方法
NS1 表达病毒和野生型病毒感染之间的免疫细胞亚群频率相当;NS1 表达不会改变亚群组成,而感染剂量则调节其丰度。
主要结果
在致死剂量感染后,肺部中性粒细胞(表达Ly-6G)显著增加,且此变化与NS1表达无关;相比之下,所有病毒的致死剂量感染均降低了CD163+和F4/80+中性粒细胞亚群。
解释与创新
致死剂量感染使肺部CD68+巨噬细胞数量增加,同时在感染后第3天减少了CD163+、CD193+和F4/80+巨噬细胞亚群及F4/80+髓样细胞数量;这些减少与NS1表达及感染剂量无关。
科研与公卫意义
在致死剂量流感A病毒感染后,NK细胞中IL-23R+和IL-12Rβ2+亚群上调,而CD193+ NK细胞亚群在感染后第3天减少。
局限与证据强度
NKT细胞分析显示,感染后第3天IL-12Rβ2+ NKT细胞亚群扩增。

Dose-Dependent Alterations in Lung Immune Subpopulations in Influenza a Virus Infection

Authors: Tatiana Betáková, Miriam Mladá, Karin Donátová, Jana Jakubíková
Original abstract (English)
This study aimed to characterize the modulation in immune cell subpopulations in murine lungs following influenza A virus (IAV) infection, assessing the effects of infectious dose, viral adaptation, and NS1 expression. Immune cell subsets were profiled by surface receptor expression using multiparametric flow cytometry with a 10-antibody immunophenotyping panel. Neutrophils expressing Ly-6G were significantly increased in the lungs following lethal-dose infection with IAV, independently of NS1 expression; in contrast, lethal-dose infection with all viruses reduced CD163+ and F4/80+ neutrophil subpopulations. Lethal-dose infection increased pulmonary CD68+ macrophages while decreasing CD163+, CD193+, and F4/80+ macrophage subsets, as well as F4/80+ myeloid cells, by day 3 post-infection; these reductions were independent of NS1 expression and infectious dose. Following lethal-dose IAV infection, NK cells exhibited upregulation of IL-23R+ and IL-12Rβ2+ subsets, while the CD193+ NK subpopulation was decreased on day 3 post-infection. Profiling of NKT cells revealed an expansion of the IL-12Rβ2+ NKT subset on day 3 post-infection. Adaptive immune profiling of lung CD4+ T cells revealed a selective increase in Th1-like cells (IL-12Rβ2+ CD4+) after WSN infection, a marked reduction in Th2-like cells (CD193+ CD4+) following infection with IAV regardless of NS1 status or dose, and an expansion of CD4+NK1.1+ cells only after lethal-dose infection. Immune cell subset frequencies were comparable between infections with NS1-expressing and wild-type viruses; NS1 expression did not alter subset composition, whereas the infection dose modulated their abundance. These findings expand our understanding of the subpopulation of immune cells and their possible role in influenza virus pathogenesis.

甲型流感病毒通过启动子依赖性核输出机制选择基因组

Influenza A virus initiates genome selection through promoter-dependent nuclear export
作者： Kuang-Yu Chen, Alison Rep, Loïc Carrique, Fangzheng Wang, Ecco Staller, Jonathan M. Grimes, Ervin Fodor
摘要中文翻译摘要 甲型流感病毒在病毒核糖核蛋白(vRNP)复合体环境下于细胞核内复制其分节段RNA 基因组。新合成的vRNP被输出至细胞质以进行病毒颗粒组装,而结构相似的互补RNP(cRNP,作为复制中间体)则不会被包装入病毒颗粒。这一选择性的分子基础仍不清楚。本研究结合启动子突变、单分子荧光原位杂交(smFISH)、病毒样颗粒(VLP)检测及冷冻电子显微镜(cryo-EM)技术,发现cRNP被滞留于细胞核内,且其输出能力由启动子结构编码,其中单链3′启动子区域的长度是主要贡献因素,而启动子序列与双链结构进一步调控输出效率。携带输出能力启动子的cRNP可被包装入病毒样颗粒,证明核输出与包装能力之间存在功能关联。冷冻电子显微镜分析显示,3′ vRNA与cRNA启动子均可结合聚合酶的同一表面位点,但与不同的聚合酶构象景观相关,仅vRNA启动子与聚合酶的包装酶构象相兼容。综上,本研究识别出一个启动子依赖性检查点,将RNA启动子结构与聚合酶构象与RNP输出及包装联系起来。这些结果表明甲型流感病毒在细胞核内启动基因组选择,并揭示核输出是基因组选择的早期步骤。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在阐明甲型流感病毒基因组复制与包装选择性的分子基础,因该机制仍不清楚。
设计与对象
该研究结合了启动子诱变、单分子荧光原位杂交 (smFISH)、病毒样颗粒 (VLP) 测定和冷冻电子显微镜 (cryo-EM),以研究新合成的 vRNP 和 cRNP 的输出能力。
核心方法
研究人员采用启动子突变、单分子荧光原位杂交(smFISH)、病毒样颗粒(VLP)检测及冷冻电子显微镜(cryo-EM)技术,探究启动子结构与RNA核输出之间的关系。
主要结果
携带输出能力启动子的cRNP可被包装入病毒样颗粒,证明核输出与包装能力之间存在功能关联。
解释与创新
研究结果揭示核输出是基因组选择的早期步骤,并识别出一个启动子依赖性检查点,将RNA启动子结构与聚合酶构象与RNP输出及包装联系起来。
科研与公卫意义
该结果为甲型流感病毒基因组选择的分子机制提供了见解,有助于指导监测、诊断及抗病毒药物策略的制定。
局限与证据强度
本研究基于摘要层面的证据,限制了分析深度,但研究结果由多种实验方法支持,整体置信度为中等。

Influenza A virus initiates genome selection through promoter-dependent nuclear export

Authors: Kuang-Yu Chen, Alison Rep, Loïc Carrique, Fangzheng Wang, Ecco Staller, Jonathan M. Grimes, Ervin Fodor
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Influenza A virus replicates its segmented RNA genome in the nucleus in the context of viral ribonucleoprotein (vRNP) complexes. Newly synthesised vRNPs are exported to the cytoplasm for virion assembly, whereas structurally similar complementary RNPs (cRNPs), which function as replication intermediates, are not incorporated into virions. The molecular basis for this selectivity remains unclear. Here, combining promoter mutagenesis, single-molecule fluorescence in situ hybridisation (smFISH), virus-like particle (VLP) assays and cryo-electron microscopy (cryo-EM), we find that cRNPs are retained in the nucleus and that export competence is encoded by promoter architecture, with the length of the single-stranded 3′ promoter region as the principal contributing feature and promoter sequence and duplex architecture further modulating export efficiency. cRNPs carrying export-competent promoters are incorporated into virus-like particles, demonstrating a functional link between nuclear export and packaging competence. Cryo-EM analyses reveal that both 3′ vRNA and cRNA promoters bind a common polymerase surface site but are associated with distinct polymerase conformational landscapes, with only the vRNA promoter being compatible with an encapsidase conformation of the polymerase. Together, our findings identify a promoter-dependent checkpoint that links RNA promoter architecture and polymerase conformation to RNP export and packaging. These results demonstrate that influenza virus initiates genome selection in the nucleus and reveal nuclear export as an early step in genome selection.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand the molecular basis for the selectivity of influenza A virus genome replication and packaging, as the mechanism remains unclear.
Design and population
The study combined promoter mutagenesis, single-molecule fluorescence in situ hybridisation (smFISH), virus-like particle (VLP) assays and cryo-electron microscopy (cryo-EM) to investigate the export competence of newly synthesised vRNPs and cRNPs.
Core methods
The researchers used promoter mutagenesis, single-molecule fluorescence in situ hybridisation (smFISH), virus-like particle (VLP) assays and cryo-electron microscopy (cryo-EM) to examine the relationship between promoter architecture and nuclear export of RNA.
Main results
cRNPs carrying export-competent promoters were incorporated into virus-like particles, showing a functional link between nuclear export and packaging competence.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings reveal that nuclear export is an early step in genome selection and identify a promoter-dependent checkpoint that links RNA promoter architecture and polymerase conformation to RNP export and packaging.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results provide insights into the molecular mechanisms of genome selection in influenza viruses, which could inform surveillance, diagnosis and development of antiviral strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on abstract-only evidence, which limits the depth of analysis, but the findings are supported by multiple experimental approaches and are considered to have moderate confidence.

AS01、AS03与明矾对GMP级H5N1抗原在豚鼠体内的免疫应答的佐剂效应比较

Comparison of the adjuvant effect of AS01, AS03 and alum on the immune response to GMP-grade H5N1 antigen in guinea pigs
作者： Emmelie Eckhardt, Josephine Friedrich, Wiebke Lange, Julia Sehl‐Ewert, Timm Harder, Jens Teifke, Ger van Zandbergen, Max Bastian
摘要中文翻译佐剂作为免疫应答的增强剂和激活剂,对疫苗的效力具有至关重要的作用。AS01是一种含有皂苷衍生物QS-21和脱毒脂多糖MPL-A的脂质体制剂,而AS03则是一种含有抗氧化剂生育酚的角鲨烯基油水乳剂,这两种佐剂均为现代高效佐剂,已成为创新型疫苗的重要组成部分。在本研究中,豚鼠(一种用于人类流感感染的经典小动物模型)通过皮下接种含有AS01、AS03、氢氧化铝或未经佐剂处理的H5N1流感抗原进行免疫。随后评估了不同佐剂处理下针对流感抗原的T细胞和B细胞的诱导情况。所有佐剂处理组均观察到针对H5N1的T细胞应答,但在6个月后,AS01和AS03免疫组的应答水平最高。流感特异性抗体滴度在AS03免疫豚鼠中最高。值得注意的是,AS03和AS01佐剂处理的流感抗原还诱导了可交叉反应的抗体,可抑制相关H5N3流感毒株(但不包括远缘H5N8流感毒株)。同时,接受卵生产的H5N1裂解抗原与AS03组合的动物产生了针对鸡成纤维细胞的异喀性、调理作用抗体。未观察到对人成纤维细胞的反应性。综上所述,本研究结果证实了AS03和AS01相较于传统明矾或无佐剂处理的佐剂效应更为优越。AS03佐剂处理后出现异喀性抗体是一个意外发现。未来需进一步研究以评估该发现的意义并阐明其机制。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在比较AS01、AS03与明矾对GMP级H5N1抗原在豚鼠体内的免疫应答的佐剂效应,因佐剂对疫苗的效力至关重要。
设计与对象
本研究采用豚鼠作为人类流感感染的模型,并通过皮下接种含有AS01、AS03、氢氧化铝或未经佐剂处理的H5N1抗原进行免疫。
核心方法
基于抗原处理方式,评估了针对流感抗原的T细胞和B细胞的诱导情况。
主要结果
所有佐剂处理组均观察到针对H5N1的T细胞应答,但在6个月后,AS01和AS03免疫组的应答水平最高。流感特异性抗体滴度在AS03免疫豚鼠中最高。
解释与创新
研究结果证实了AS03和AS01相较于传统明矾或无佐剂处理的佐剂效应更为优越。
科研与公卫意义
本研究结果可为开发更有效的疫苗提供参考,通过强调AS01和AS03作为佐剂的潜力,为季节性流感的疫苗研发提供科学依据。
局限与证据强度
本研究基于动物模型,未提供直接的人体数据,但通过明确的佐剂处理对比,证据强度较高。

Comparison of the adjuvant effect of AS01, AS03 and alum on the immune response to GMP-grade H5N1 antigen in guinea pigs

Authors: Emmelie Eckhardt, Josephine Friedrich, Wiebke Lange, Julia Sehl‐Ewert, Timm Harder, Jens Teifke, Ger van Zandbergen, Max Bastian
Original abstract (English)
Adjuvants function as an enhancer and activator of the immune response and can be of pivotal importance for the efficacy of vaccines. AS01, which is a liposome formulation spiked with the saponin derivate QS-21 and the detoxified lipopolysaccharide MPL-A, and AS03, which is a squalene-based oil in water emulsion that contains antioxidant tocopherol, are modern, highly potent adjuvants that became integral to innovative vaccines. In the current study, guinea pigs -a well-established small animal model for human influenza-infections- were subcutaneously vaccinated with an influenza H5N1-antigen formulated with AS01, AS03, aluminium hydroxide or left unformulated. The induction of T- and B cells specific for the influenza antigen was assessed in dependence of the antigen formulation. H5N1-specific T cell responses were observed with all adjuvant formulations, but after six months were highest in the AS01- and AS03-immunized groups. The influenza specific antibody titres were highest in AS03-vaccinated guinea pigs. Importantly, AS03- and AS01-formulated influenza antigen also induced crossreactive antibodies inhibiting related H5N3- but not a distant H5N8-influenza strain. At the same time, it was observed that animals, which received the egg-produced H5N1 split antigen in combination with AS03, mounted xenoreactive, opsonizing antibodies that bound to chicken fibroblasts. No reactivity was observed to human fibroblasts. In summary, our results confirm the superior adjuvanticity of AS03 and of AS01 as compared to conventional alum or non-adjuvanted formulations. The presence of xenoreactive antibodies after AS03 vaccination was an unexpected observation. Further investigations will be required to appreciate the significance and identify the mechanisms of this findings.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to compare the adjuvant effect of AS01, AS03 and alum on the immune response to GMP-grade H5N1 antigen in guinea pigs, as adjuvants are crucial for vaccine efficacy.
Design and population
The study used guinea pigs as a model for human influenza infections and vaccinated them with an H5N1 antigen formulated with AS01, AS03, aluminium hydroxide or left unformulated.
Core methods
The induction of T- and B cells specific for the influenza antigen was assessed based on the antigen formulation.
Main results
H5N1-specific T cell responses were observed with all adjuvant formulations, but after six months were highest in the AS01- and AS03-immunized groups. The influenza-specific antibody titres were highest in AS03-vaccinated guinea pigs.
Interpretation and novelty
The results confirm the superior adjuvanticity of AS03 and AS01 compared to conventional alum or non-adjuvanted formulations.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform the development of more effective vaccines for seasonal influenza by highlighting the potential of AS01 and AS03 as adjuvants.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to an animal model and does not provide direct human data, but the evidence is strong due to the clear comparison of adjuvant formulations.
