流感A型含有缺失的病毒基因组的生成受到代谢物、代谢信号分子和蓝藻提取物的影响

Influenza A deletion-containing viral genome production is altered by metabolites, metabolic signaling molecules, and cyanobacterial extracts
作者： Ilechukwu Agu, Suzanna Y. Gomez, Ivy R. José, Samuel L. Díaz-Muñoz, Samuel L. Díaz‐Muñoz
摘要中文翻译摘要 RNA病毒感染会产生大量非标准病毒基因组,这些病毒与较轻的临床结果相关,因此引发了研究利用它们作为新型抗病毒药物的探索。我们最近发现,使用阿来匹利(alpelisib)改变宿主细胞代谢,使其偏离促生长通路,可以显著增加流感A型非标准病毒基因组(特别是含有缺失的病毒基因组,DelVGs)的生成。为了发现更多能够诱导DelVGs生成的药物,我们对流感感染(A/H1N1和A/H3N2)进行了代谢物、代谢信号分子和蓝藻衍生生物制剂的筛选,并使用长读长测序技术量化了DelVGs和总病毒基因组。我们发现,宿主细胞中心碳代谢产生的代谢物和信号分子可以显著改变流感A型感染早期DelVGs的生成。腺苷被鉴定为一种强效的缺陷病毒基因组诱导剂,能够显著增强两种亚型的DelVGs生成。胰岛素也有类似效果,但具有亚型特异性,主要增强A/H3N2感染中聚合酶片段的DelVGs生成。三羧酸(TCA)循环抑制剂4-辛基衣康酸和UK5099,以及嘌呤类似物 Favipiravir,能够增强两种亚型的总病毒基因组生成。蓝藻提取物主要影响A/H3N2感染中DelVGs和总病毒基因组的生成,特别是几乎完全抑制了流感抗原片段的生成。这些结果表明,针对宿主代谢通路(PI3K-AKT、Ras-MAPK通路、TCA和嘌呤-嘧啶通路)以及影响DelVGs生成的蓝藻治疗药物,可能为抗病毒药物干预提供新的途径。我们的研究加深了对DelVGs生成机制的基本理解,并突出了通过靶向宿主代谢来开发广谱流感治疗药物的潜力。重要性 目前用于流感感染的抗病毒药物缺乏有效性或面临快速出现的抗病毒药物耐药性问题。流感感染自然会产生非标准病毒基因组,这些病毒干扰病毒繁殖并导致较轻的临床结果。在这里,我们测试了能够改变宿主代谢的药物,以诱导流感感染产生更多的这种“天然抗病毒药物”。我们发现,由人类代谢产生的分子,特别是腺苷,可以增加这些含有缺失的病毒基因组(DelVGs)的生成,并且还发现蓝藻提取物具有抗病毒药物效应。我们的发现表明,观察到的缺陷病毒和全长病毒基因组之间的社会互动不仅取决于病毒本身,还可能受到宿主提供的阶段的影响。我们的发现可能在增加DelVGs生成用于研究或工业用途、利用代谢物作为流感感染严重程度的生物标志物以及使用药物改变宿主代谢以引导流感感染向较轻结果发展方面具有潜在应用价值。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在探讨如何通过改变宿主细胞代谢来影响流感感染中非标准病毒基因组的生成,因为这些基因组已被与较轻的临床结果相关联。研究的动机源于当前流感治疗策略的局限性,需要新的抗病毒药物方法。
设计与对象
本研究对流感感染(A/H1N1和A/H3N2)进行了代谢物、代谢信号分子和蓝藻提取物的筛选,以识别能够增加含有缺失的病毒基因组(DelVGs)生成的物质。研究使用长读长测序技术来量化DelVGs和总病毒基因组。
核心方法
研究人员对流感感染(A/H1N1和A/H3N2)进行了代谢物、代谢信号分子和蓝藻提取物的筛选。他们使用长读长测序技术量化DelVGs和总病毒基因组,以评估这些物质对病毒基因组生成的影响。
主要结果
宿主细胞中心碳代谢产生的代谢物和信号分子在流感A型感染早期显著改变了DelVGs的生成。腺苷被鉴定为一种强效的DelVGs诱导剂,能够增强两种亚型的DelVGs生成。TCA循环抑制剂和嘌呤类似物Favipiravir能够增强两种亚型的总病毒基因组生成。
解释与创新
这些发现表明,宿主代谢通路,如PI3K-AKT和Ras-MAPK通路,可以作为靶点以增加DelVGs的生成,从而提供新的抗病毒药物策略。本研究突出了利用代谢物和蓝藻提取物来影响病毒基因组生成的潜力。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果揭示了DelVGs生成的机制,并表明靶向宿主代谢可能有助于开发广谱流感治疗药物,从而将感染引导至较轻的临床结果。
局限与证据强度
本研究仅限于抽象层面的证据,其发现基于体外或体内模型,而非临床数据。证据强度中等,因为结果来源于筛选方法,缺乏详细的机制或临床验证。

Influenza A deletion-containing viral genome production is altered by metabolites, metabolic signaling molecules, and cyanobacterial extracts

Authors: Ilechukwu Agu, Suzanna Y. Gomez, Ivy R. José, Samuel L. Díaz-Muñoz, Samuel L. Díaz‐Muñoz
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT RNA virus infections generate abundant non-standard viral genomes that have been associated with milder clinical outcomes, leading to research into harnessing them as novel antivirals. We recently showed that altering host cell metabolism away from pro-growth pathways using alpelisib increased the production of influenza A nonstandard viral genomes (specifically deletion-containing viral genomes, DelVGs). To uncover more drugs that could induce increased DelVGs, we subjected influenza infections (A/H1N1 and A/H3N2) to a screen of metabolites, metabolic signaling molecules, and cyanobacterium-derived biologics, and quantified DelVGs and total viral genomes using long-read sequencing. We show that metabolites and signaling molecules of host cell central carbon metabolism can significantly alter DelVG production early in influenza A infection. Adenosine emerged as a potent inducer of defective viral genomes, significantly amplifying DelVG production across both subtypes. Insulin had similar effects, albeit subtype-specific, predominantly enhancing polymerase segment DelVGs in A/H3N2 infections. Tricarboxylic acid (TCA) cycle inhibitors, 4-octyl itaconate and UK5099, along with the purine analog favipiravir, increased total viral genome production across subtypes. Cyanobacterial extracts primarily affected DelVGs and total viral genome production in A/H3N2 infections, with a specific, almost complete shutdown of influenza antigenic segments. These results suggest new avenues for antiviral intervention, including host metabolic pathways (PI3K-AKT, Ras-MAPK pathways, TCA, and purine-pyrimidine) and cyanotherapeutics that affect DelVG production. Our study advances our fundamental understanding of DelVG production mechanisms and highlights the potential of targeting host metabolism to develop broad-spectrum influenza therapeutics. IMPORTANCE Antivirals currently available for flu infections lack effectiveness or face rapid emergence of antiviral resistance. Flu infections naturally produce nonstandard viral genomes, which interfere with viral reproduction and lead to milder clinical outcomes. Here, we test drugs that alter the host metabolism to induce influenza infections to produce more of this “natural antiviral.” We find that molecules generated from human metabolism, particularly adenosine, can increase these deletion-containing viral genomes (DelVGs) and also find evidence that biological extracts from cyanobacteria have antiviral effects. Our findings suggest that the social interactions observed between defective and full-length viral genomes depend not only on the viral actors but can also be altered by the stage provided by the host. Our findings have potential applications to increasing DelVG production for research or industrial uses; employing metabolites as biomarkers for flu infection severity; and using drugs to alter host metabolism to steer influenza infections to milder outcomes.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to explore how altering host cell metabolism could influence the production of nonstandard viral genomes in influenza infections, as these genomes have been linked to milder clinical outcomes. The research was driven by the need for new antiviral strategies, given the limitations of current flu treatments.
Design and population
The study involved screening influenza infections (A/H1N1 and A/H3N2) for metabolites, metabolic signaling molecules, and cyanobacterial extracts to identify agents that could increase the production of deletion-containing viral genomes (DelVGs). The research was conducted using long-read sequencing to quantify DelVGs and total viral genomes.
Core methods
The researchers performed a screen of metabolites, metabolic signaling molecules, and cyanobacterial extracts on influenza infections (A/H1N1 and A/H3N2). They quantified DelVGs and total viral genomes using long-read sequencing to assess the impact of these agents on viral genome production.
Main results
Metabolites and signaling molecules from host central carbon metabolism significantly altered DelVG production early in influenza A infection. Adenosine was identified as a potent inducer of DelVGs, increasing their production across both subtypes. TCA cycle inhibitors and the purine analog favipiravir increased total viral genome production across subtypes.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that host metabolic pathways, such as the PI3K-AKT and Ras-MAPK pathways, can be targeted to increase DelVG production, offering new antiviral strategies. The study highlights the potential of using metabolites and cyanobacterial extracts to influence viral genome production.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results provide insights into the mechanisms of DelVG production and suggest that targeting host metabolism could lead to the development of broad-spectrum influenza therapeutics, potentially steering infections toward milder outcomes.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to abstract-level evidence, and the findings are based on in vitro or in vivo models rather than clinical data. The evidence strength is moderate, as the results are derived from a screening approach without detailed mechanistic or clinical validation.

科威特孕妇对流感疫苗接种的认知、态度与认知:一项描述性横断面研究

Knowledge, attitudes and perceptions about influenza vaccination among pregnant women in Kuwait: a descriptive cross-sectional study
作者： Fatemah Alhendyani, Louise Hayes, Laura Lindsey, Richard McNally
摘要中文翻译摘要 孕妇流感疫苗接种(MIV)是预防孕妇严重流感相关并发症最有效的措施。然而,科威特孕妇MIV接种情况的相关知识仍有限。本研究旨在评估科威特孕妇对MIV接种及提供情况的认知、态度与认知。本研究采用横断面医院调查设计,使用世界卫生组织(WHO)「定制免疫规划(TIP FLU)」问卷改编的自填电子问卷,于2022年10月至2023年1月期间对科威特政府产科医院及产前门诊就诊的272名孕妇及近期分娩妇女进行调查。仅22名参与者(8.1%)报告在当前或最近一次妊娠期间接种了MIV。尽管大多数参与者认识到孕期流感感染可能导致严重后果,但仅8%的参与者达到MIV认知充分的预定义阈值。医疗专业人员对流感感染的认知显著高于其他职业参与者(p=0.012),而年龄较小的参与者对MIV的认知显著更高(p=0.023)。参与者特征与MIV接种率之间未发现显著关联。参与者认为医疗服务提供者的推荐是影响疫苗接种决策的重要因素,但许多人报告在孕期未获得此类推荐。科威特孕妇对孕妇流感疫苗接种及疫苗接种的认知水平较低。研究结果凸显加强产前教育及鼓励医疗服务提供者在孕期持续讨论并推荐孕妇流感疫苗接种的机会。需开展样本量更大的进一步研究以识别疫苗接种的独立预测因子。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
季节性孕妇流感疫苗接种(MIV)被推荐用于预防孕期严重流感相关并发症,但科威特的接种情况仍鲜有记录。本研究旨在填补科威特孕妇对MIV提供及接种的认知、态度与认知的知识空白。
设计与对象
本研究采用描述性横断面医院调查设计,于2022年10月至2023年1月期间对科威特政府产科医院及产前门诊就诊的272名孕妇及近期分娩妇女进行调查,使用世界卫生组织「定制免疫规划(TIP FLU)」工具改编的自填电子问卷收集数据。
核心方法
本研究使用世界卫生组织TIP FLU工具改编的自填电子问卷,评估参与者对MIV的认知、态度与认知。数据于指定期间在政府产科医院及产前门诊收集。
主要结果
仅22名参与者(8.1%)报告在当前或最近一次妊娠期间接种了MIV。尽管大多数参与者认识到孕期流感感染可能导致严重后果,但仅8%的参与者达到MIV认知充分的预定义阈值。
解释与创新
作者将MIV接种率及认知水平低解释为产前教育及医疗服务提供者推荐机会的缺失。研究强调需加强产前教育及医疗服务提供者的持续参与以提升疫苗接种接种率。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,针对性产前教育及常规医疗服务提供者推荐可能提升科威特MIV接种率,从而潜在降低孕妇流感相关发病率。
局限与证据强度
本研究的横断面设计及对自报数据的依赖限制了因果推断,可能低估真实疫苗接种率。272名参与者的样本量及缺乏多变量分析降低了证据强度,需要更大规模研究以识别接种的预测因子。

Knowledge, attitudes and perceptions about influenza vaccination among pregnant women in Kuwait: a descriptive cross-sectional study

Authors: Fatemah Alhendyani, Louise Hayes, Laura Lindsey, Richard McNally
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Maternal influenza vaccination (MIV) is the most effective measure of preventing severe influenza-related complications in pregnant women. However, there is limited knowledge about MIV uptake in Kuwait. This study aimed to assess pregnant women’s knowledge, attitudes, and perceptions regarding MIV uptake and provision in Kuwait. A cross-sectional, hospital-based study using a self-administered electronic questionnaire adapted from the World Health Organization (WHO) Tailoring Immunization Programmes (TIP FLU) questionnaire was conducted among 272 pregnant and recently delivered women attending governmental maternity hospitals and antenatal clinics in Kuwait between October 2022 and January 2023. Only 22 participants (8.1%) reported receiving MIV during their current or most recent pregnancy. Although most participants recognised that influenza infection could have serious consequences during pregnancy, only 8% met the predefined threshold for adequate knowledge regarding MIV. Healthcare professionals demonstrated significantly higher knowledge of influenza infection than participants from other professions ( p = 0.012), while younger participants had significantly greater knowledge of MIV ( p = 0.023). No significant associations were identified between participant characteristics and MIV uptake. Participants recognised healthcare provider recommendation as an important factor influencing vaccination decisions, although many reported not receiving such a recommendation during pregnancy. Knowledge of maternal influenza vaccination and vaccine uptake were low among pregnant women in Kuwait. These findings highlight opportunities to strengthen antenatal education and encourage healthcare providers to consistently discuss and recommend maternal influenza vaccination during pregnancy. Further studies with larger samples are needed to identify independent predictors of vaccine uptake.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Seasonal maternal influenza vaccination (MIV) is recommended to prevent severe influenza-related complications in pregnancy, yet uptake in Kuwait remains poorly documented. The study addressed a knowledge gap on pregnant women’s understanding, attitudes, and perceptions regarding MIV provision and uptake in Kuwait.
Design and population
A descriptive, cross-sectional, hospital-based study was conducted among 272 pregnant and recently delivered women attending governmental maternity hospitals and antenatal clinics in Kuwait between October 2022 and January 2023 using a self-administered electronic questionnaire adapted from the WHO Tailoring Immunization Programmes (TIP FLU) tool.
Core methods
The study used a self-administered electronic questionnaire adapted from the WHO TIP FLU tool to assess knowledge, attitudes, and perceptions about MIV among participants. Data were collected from governmental maternity hospitals and antenatal clinics during the specified period.
Main results
Only 22 participants (8.1%) reported receiving MIV during their current or most recent pregnancy. Although most participants recognized that influenza infection could have serious consequences during pregnancy, only 8% met the predefined threshold for adequate knowledge regarding MIV.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the low MIV uptake and knowledge as indicating missed opportunities for antenatal education and provider recommendation. They highlighted the need for stronger antenatal education and consistent healthcare provider engagement to improve vaccination uptake.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that targeted antenatal education and routine healthcare provider recommendations could improve MIV uptake in Kuwait, potentially reducing influenza-related morbidity in pregnant women.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study’s cross-sectional design and reliance on self-reported data limit causal inference and may underestimate true vaccine uptake. The sample size of 272 participants and lack of multivariable analysis reduce the strength of evidence, warranting larger studies to identify predictors of uptake.

快速磁珠核酸提取提升流感RT-qPCR灵敏度及分型成功率

Rapid magnetic bead nucleic acid extraction enhances influenza RT-qPCR sensitivity and subtyping success
作者： Matthew L. Cavuto, Suleyman Sarp Pinar, Javier Sánchez-Martínez, Carla Rodríguez-Crespo, Ivana Pennisi, Katarzyna Szostak-Lipowicz, Nicolas Moser, Kenny Malpartida-Cardenas, Alison Holmes, José María Eiros
摘要中文翻译核酸提取仍是分子流感检测在非中心化实验室开展的主要基础设施瓶颈,因为磁珠纯化通常依赖于市电供电的提取仪和经过培训的操作人员。我们在2024–2025流感季期间,于一家国家流感中心对311份鼻咽拭子标本,评估了SmartLid(一种无离心格式,其中可移除的磁性钥匙可在预分装的裂解/结合、洗涤和洗脱缓冲液中转运顺磁珠,无需移液或电动设备)与自动磁珠提取仪(Nextractor NX-48S)的性能。将两种方法提取的洗脱液在相同单重RT-qPCR条件下扩增流感甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09、甲型H3N2流感病毒及乙型Victoria系流感病毒。两种方法均实现100%特异性(47/47阴性;无假阳性)。SmartLid提取后,分型在264份参考阳性标本中成功263份,而自动提取后成功241份(99.62% vs 91.29%;差异8.33个百分点;不一致样本对23 vs 1;McNemar检验P < 0.001)。在240份完整配对样本中,SmartLid提取后的循环阈值(Ct)值更低(配对中位数差值-2.78个循环;估计位置偏移-2.60个循环,95%置信区间-2.82至-2.37;P < 0.001),且标本在两种方法间的排序保持一致(Spearman rho = 0.84)。该优势在三种亚型及新鲜与冰冻标本中均保持(校正P < 0.001)。仅在SmartLid提取后检测到的标本,其Ct值高于双检标本(中位数34.37 vs 28.54;P < 0.001),表明增益出现在检测限附近。因此,无仪器手动格式可超越自动参考工作流程的提取效率,使质量保证的流感分型扩展至非中心化实验室。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
核酸提取仍是分散化分子流感检测的主要障碍,因磁珠纯化通常需依赖市电供电的提取仪和经过培训的操作人员,限制了非中心化实验室的开展。
设计与对象
在一家国家流感中心,采用2024–2025流感季收集的311份鼻咽拭子标本,比较无离心磁珠提取方法SmartLid与自动磁珠提取仪(Nextractor NX-48S)的性能。
核心方法
将两种提取方法获得的配对洗脱液,在相同单重RT-qPCR条件下扩增流感甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09、甲型H3N2流感病毒及乙型Victoria系流感病毒,记录分型成功率与循环阈值(Ct)值。
主要结果
SmartLid的分型成功率为99.62%(263/264),自动提取仪为91.29%(241/264),差异为8.33个百分点(McNemar检验P < 0.001);SmartLid提取后的Ct值更低(配对中位数差值-2.78个循环;估计位置偏移-2.60个循环,95%置信区间-2.82至-2.37;P < 0.001),且标本排序保持一致(Spearman rho = 0.84)。
解释与创新
作者将结果解释为表明无仪器手动提取格式可超越自动参考工作流程的效率,使质量保证的流感分型扩展至非中心化实验室。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明SmartLid有望使流感分子检测去中心化,提升及时分型的可及性,并支持季节性流感监测与临床决策制定,尤其在资源受限环境中。
局限与证据强度
该研究在单一国家流感中心进行,标本集来自一个流感季,限制了结果的普遍性;但强统计显著性及在各亚型与标本类型间的一致性,支持证据强度为中等。

Rapid magnetic bead nucleic acid extraction enhances influenza RT-qPCR sensitivity and subtyping success

Authors: Matthew L. Cavuto, Suleyman Sarp Pinar, Javier Sánchez-Martínez, Carla Rodríguez-Crespo, Ivana Pennisi, Katarzyna Szostak-Lipowicz, Nicolas Moser, Kenny Malpartida-Cardenas, Alison Holmes, José María Eiros
Original abstract (English)
Nucleic acid extraction remains the principal infrastructure barrier to molecular influenza testing outside centralised laboratories, since bead-based purification is normally tied to mains-powered extractors and trained operators. We evaluated SmartLid, a centrifugation-free format in which a removable magnetic key shuttles paramagnetic beads through pre-aliquoted lysis/binding, wash, and elution buffers without pipetting or powered instrumentation, against an automated magnetic-bead extractor (Nextractor NX-48S) on 311 nasopharyngeal specimens from the 2024-2025 influenza season at a National Influenza Centre. Paired eluates were amplified under identical monoplex RT-qPCR conditions for influenza A(H1N1)pdm09, A(H3), and B/Victoria. Both methods gave 100% specificity (47/47 negatives; no false positives). Subtyping succeeded in 263/264 reference-positive specimens after SmartLid extraction versus 241/264 after automated extraction (99.62% versus 91.29%; difference 8.33 percentage points; discordant pairs 23 versus 1; McNemar P < 0.001). Across 240 complete pairs, cycle threshold (Ct) values were lower after SmartLid extraction (median paired difference -2.78 cycles; estimated location shift -2.60 cycles, 95% CI -2.82 to -2.37; P < 0.001) with rank-ordering of specimens conserved between methods (Spearman rho = 0.84). The advantage was preserved across all three subtypes and in both fresh and frozen specimens (adjusted P < 0.001). Specimens recovered only after SmartLid extraction had higher Ct values than dual-detected specimens (median 34.37 versus 28.54; P < 0.001), locating the gain near the assay detection limit. An instrument-free manual format can therefore exceed the extraction efficiency of an automated reference workflow, extending quality-assured influenza subtyping beyond centralised laboratories.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Nucleic acid extraction remains a major barrier to decentralised molecular influenza testing because bead-based purification typically requires mains-powered extractors and trained operators, limiting access to centralised laboratories.
Design and population
A comparative evaluation was conducted at a National Influenza Centre using 311 nasopharyngeal specimens collected during the 2024–2025 influenza season, comparing SmartLid, a centrifugation-free magnetic bead extraction method, against an automated magnetic-bead extractor (Nextractor NX-48S).
Core methods
Paired eluates from both extraction methods were amplified under identical monoplex RT-qPCR conditions for influenza A(H1N1)pdm09, A(H3), and B/Victoria, with subtyping success and cycle threshold (Ct) values recorded.
Main results
SmartLid achieved 99.62% subtyping success (263/264) versus 91.29% (241/264) for the automated extractor (difference 8.33 percentage points; McNemar P < 0.001), with lower Ct values (median paired difference -2.78 cycles; estimated location shift -2.60 cycles, 95% CI -2.82 to -2.37; P < 0.001) and preserved rank-ordering (Spearman rho = 0.84).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as demonstrating that an instrument-free manual extraction format can exceed the efficiency of an automated reference workflow, enabling quality-assured influenza subtyping beyond centralised laboratories.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that SmartLid could decentralise influenza molecular testing, improving access to timely subtyping and supporting surveillance and clinical decision-making in resource-limited settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was conducted at a single National Influenza Centre with a specific specimen set from one season, limiting generalisability; however, the strong statistical significance and consistency across subtypes and specimen types support moderate evidence strength.

意大利北部一家大型养老院居民中甲型H3N2流感暴发的病例报告:感染率、临床特征与结局

An outbreak report of Influenza A(H3) among residents of a large nursing home in northern Italy: attack rates, clinical features and outcomes
作者： Federico Fonda, A Faruzzo, Gabriella Bellini, Antonio Provenzano, Emanuele Lampis, Laura Brunelli, Stefania Marzinotto, Corrado Pipan, Francesco Curcio, Gaetano Privitera
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 养老院是甲型流感暴发的高风险场所,2025–2026流感季在全球范围内呈现出较往年提前的流行起始特征,并出现了新型甲型流感病毒亚分支K株(包括未在2025–2026季节性流感株中检出的突变)。详细的流行病学调查对于指导这些高风险但常被忽视的场所的感染预防与控制(IPC)策略至关重要。方法 本研究为一项基于意大利北部一家375床位公立养老院持续前瞻性队列研究的病例报告。急性呼吸道感染(ARI)监测于2025年11月至12月在所有入组居民中实施。对所有ARI病例采集鼻咽拭子并检测甲型H3N2流感病毒。病例定义为出现ARI且实验室确诊为甲型H3N2流感病毒的居民。计算感染率(AR)、病例住院率(CHR)与病例死亡率(CFR),并提供95%置信区间。研究结果依据医院感染报告与干预研究ORION指南报告。结果 在217名参与居民中(中位年龄88岁,83%接种过流感疫苗),50例(感染率为23%;95%置信区间17.6–29.2)实验室确诊为甲型H3N2流感病毒。发热(89.8%)与咳嗽(81.6%)为主要症状。症状中位持续时间为3天(范围1–14天)。病例住院率为8%(4/50;95%置信区间2.2–19.2),病例死亡率为0.0%(0/50;95%置信区间0.0–7.1)。结论 本病例报告显示甲型H3N2流感病毒感染的整体感染率略低于养老院流感暴发的已发表中位数,病例住院率较低且实验室确诊病例中无确诊死亡。该暴发始于第48周,较意大利历史流行起始时间提前,凸显在传统冬季高峰前需采取主动监测、持续关注流感病毒变异株与加强长期护理机构感染预防控制措施的必要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在调查养老院中甲型H3N2流感病毒暴发,因流行起始时间较往年提前且出现未纳入季节性流感株的新型变异株,强调在高风险场所理解其临床特征与结局的必要性。
设计与对象
本研究为一项基于意大利北部一家375床位养老院持续前瞻性队列研究的病例报告,涉及2025年11月至12月的217名居民。
核心方法
对所有入组居民实施急性呼吸道感染(ARI)监测,采集鼻咽拭子并检测甲型H3N2流感病毒。病例定义为出现ARI且实验室确诊为甲型H3N2流感病毒的居民。
主要结果
在217名居民中,50例实验室确诊为甲型H3N2流感病毒,感染率为23%(95%置信区间17.6–29.2)。病例住院率为8%(95%置信区间2.2–19.2),病例死亡率为0.0%(95%置信区间0.0–7.1)。
解释与创新
作者将研究结果解读为显示甲型H3N2流感病毒感染的整体感染率略低于养老院流感暴发的已发表中位数,病例住院率较低且实验室确诊病例中无确诊死亡。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调在传统冬季高峰前需采取主动监测、持续关注流感病毒变异株与加强长期护理机构感染预防控制措施的重要性。
局限与证据强度
本研究仅为摘要报告,且基于单一养老院的流感暴发调查,可能影响结果的普遍性。对研究结果的信心程度为中等。

An outbreak report of Influenza A(H3) among residents of a large nursing home in northern Italy: attack rates, clinical features and outcomes

Authors: Federico Fonda, A Faruzzo, Gabriella Bellini, Antonio Provenzano, Emanuele Lampis, Laura Brunelli, Stefania Marzinotto, Corrado Pipan, Francesco Curcio, Gaetano Privitera
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Nursing homes are high-risk settings for Influenza outbreaks, and the 2025–2026 Influenza season has been characterised by an earlier-than-usual epidemic onset globally with the emergence of a novel Influenza A(H3N2) subclade K strain including mutations that were not present in the strains of the 2025–2026 seasonal vaccine. Detailed outbreak investigations are essential to inform Infection Prevention and Control (IPC) strategies in these high-risk yet often neglected settings. Methods This is an outbreak report originating from data collected within an ongoing prospective surveillance in a 375-bed publicly funded nursing home in northern Italy. Surveillance for acute respiratory infection (ARI) was applied to all residents enrolled in the study (November-December 2025). Nasopharyngeal swabs were collected from all ARI cases and analysed for Influenza A(H3). Cases were defined as residents with ARI and laboratory-confirmed Influenza A(H3). Attack rates (ARs), case-hospitalisation rate (CHR), and case-fatality rate (CFR) were calculated with 95% confidence intervals. Findings are here reported according to the Outbreak Reports and Intervention Studies of Nosocomial infection (ORION) guidelines. Results Of 217 involved residents (median age 88 years, 83% Influenza vaccinated), 50 (AR = 23%; 95% CI 17.6–29.2) had laboratory-confirmed Influenza A(H3). Fever (89.8%) and cough (81.6%) were the predominant symptoms. Median symptom duration was 3 days (range 1–14 days). The CHR was 8% (4/50; 95% CI 2.2–19.2) and the CFR was 0.0% (0/50; 95% CI 0.0-7.1) among confirmed cases. Conclusions This outbreak of Influenza A(H3) documented an overall attack rate slightly below the published median for nursing home outbreaks, with a low CHR and no confirmed deaths among laboratory-confirmed cases. The outbreak commenced in week 48, earlier than the historical Italian epidemic onset, underscoring the need for proactive surveillance, sustained attention to Influenza vaccination and IPC measures in long-term care facilities even ahead of the traditional winter peak.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to investigate an outbreak of Influenza A(H3) in a nursing home, highlighting the need for understanding its clinical features and outcomes in high-risk settings due to the earlier-than-usual epidemic onset and the emergence of a novel strain not included in the seasonal vaccine.
Design and population
The study is an outbreak report based on data from a prospective surveillance in a 375-bed nursing home in northern Italy, involving 217 residents during November-December 2025.
Core methods
Acute respiratory infection (ARI) surveillance was conducted among all residents, with nasopharyngeal swabs collected and tested for Influenza A(H3). Cases were defined as residents with ARI and laboratory-confirmed Influenza A(H3).
Main results
Of the 217 residents, 50 had laboratory-confirmed Influenza A(H3), resulting in an attack rate of 23% (95% CI 17.6–29.2). The case-hospitalisation rate was 8% (95% CI 2.2–19.2), and the case-fatality rate was 0.0% (95% CI 0.0–7.1).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as showing an overall attack rate slightly below the published median for nursing home outbreaks, with a low hospitalisation rate and no confirmed deaths among laboratory-confirmed cases.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the importance of proactive surveillance, sustained attention to influenza vaccination, and infection prevention and control measures in long-term care facilities, even before the traditional winter peak.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to an abstract-only report, and the evidence is based on a single nursing home outbreak, which may affect generalisability. The confidence in the findings is moderate.

科威特医护人员季节性流感疫苗接种接种模式及预测因素:一项全国多季节研究

Patterns and Predictors of Influenza Vaccination Uptake Among Healthcare Personnel in Kuwait: A Nationwide Multi-Season Study
作者： Saleh Alsarhan, Fatema Alkhulaifi, Lolwah Alzafiri
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
医护人员(HP)面临职业暴露于季节性流感的风险,但疫苗接种接种率仍不理想,这增加了医院内感染传播的风险。本研究旨在填补关于科威特医护人员流感季节性疫苗接种接种模式及预测因素的知识空白,以指导针对性干预措施。
设计与对象
本研究利用卫生部的行政记录,开展了一项全国范围的观察性研究,覆盖了2022/2023至2024/2025流感季节。人员-季节数据集包括48,886名医护人员,贡献了146,658次观察记录,独立变量涵盖了人口统计学、职业和健康相关因素。
核心方法
流感季节性疫苗接种定义为在某一季节的9月至次年3月期间接受流感疫苗。采用广义估计方程(GEE)逻辑回归模型,使用可交换的相关结构来估计与疫苗接种接种相关的因素,并调整年龄、性别、国籍、居住地的州、职业、管理职位、前一年疫苗接种接种状态以及流感季节前的病假天数。
主要结果
Overall in-season vaccination uptake was 8.7%, with repeated vaccination across seasons being uncommon. Older age, male sex, non-Kuwaiti nationality, and being a physician were independently associated with higher odds of vaccination. Previous-year vaccination was the strongest predictor (aOR 2.01, 95% CI 1.87–2.16; p < 0.001). Pre-season sickness absence showed a non-linear association: 1–3 days slightly increased odds (aOR 1.08, 95% CI 1.03–1.14), while ≥8 days decreased odds (aOR 0.91, 95% CI 0.85–0.97).
解释与创新
作者将低接种率和有限的连续性解释为科威特医护人员疫苗接种接种存在持续障碍的证据。他们强调了前一年疫苗接种接种作为预测因素的主导作用,建议干预措施应优先维持年度接种率,而非启动首次接种。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,需要采取针对性策略来提高科威特医护人员的流感季节性疫苗接种接种率,特别是针对接种率较低的年轻员工和流感季节前病假较长的群体。本研究还强调了保持疫苗接种接种记录以支持连续护理的重要性。
局限与证据强度
本研究依赖于行政记录,可能缺乏对个人态度、信念或获取障碍的详细数据。观察性设计限制了因果推论,且其结果在其他环境或人群中的适用性仍不确定。由于这些限制,证据强度被评估为中等。

Patterns and Predictors of Influenza Vaccination Uptake Among Healthcare Personnel in Kuwait: A Nationwide Multi-Season Study

Authors: Saleh Alsarhan, Fatema Alkhulaifi, Lolwah Alzafiri
Original abstract (English)
Background/Objectives: Healthcare personnel (HP) are a priority group for seasonal influenza vaccination due to their increased occupational exposure and potential to transmit influenza in healthcare settings. This study examined patterns and factors associated with in-season influenza vaccination uptake among HP in Kuwait. Methods: A nationwide observational study was conducted using Ministry of Health (MOH) administrative records covering the 2022/2023 through 2024/2025 influenza seasons. A person–season dataset was constructed, comprising one observation per HP for each of the three seasons. The primary outcome was in-season influenza vaccination, defined as receipt of an influenza vaccine between September and March of a given study season. Independent variables included age, sex, nationality, governorate of residence, profession, managerial position, previous-year influenza vaccination status, and pre-season sickness absence duration (days). Generalized estimating equations (GEE) logistic regression model with an exchangeable correlation structure was used to estimate factors associated with vaccination uptake. Results: The final analytic dataset included 48,886 HP contributing 146,658 person–season observations across three influenza seasons. Overall, in-season vaccination uptake was 8.7%, and repeated vaccination across seasons was uncommon. Older age, male sex, non-Kuwaiti nationality, and being a physician were independently associated with higher odds of in-season influenza vaccination. Previous-year influenza vaccination was the strongest predictor of current-season vaccination uptake (aOR 2.01, 95% CI 1.87–2.16; p < 0.001). Compared with HP with no pre-season sickness absence, those with 1–3 pre-season sickness absence days had slightly higher adjusted odds of vaccination (aOR 1.08, 95% CI 1.03–1.14), whereas those with ≥8 days had lower adjusted odds (aOR 0.91, 95% CI 0.85–0.97). Conclusions: In-season influenza vaccination uptake among HP in Kuwait was low across three consecutive influenza seasons, with limited continuity of annual vaccination. Previous-year vaccination was the strongest predictor of current-season vaccination uptake, while the association between pre-season sickness absence and vaccination varied by absence duration. These findings support continued efforts to improve and sustain in-season influenza vaccination uptake among HP, while further research is needed to clarify the factors underlying vaccination decisions.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Healthcare personnel (HP) face occupational exposure to seasonal influenza, yet vaccination uptake remains suboptimal, creating a risk for nosocomial transmission. The study addressed the knowledge gap on patterns and predictors of in-season influenza vaccination among HP in Kuwait to inform targeted interventions.
Design and population
A nationwide observational study was conducted using Ministry of Health administrative records spanning the 2022/2023 through 2024/2025 influenza seasons. A person–season dataset included 48,886 HP contributing 146,658 observations, with independent variables covering demographic, occupational, and health-related factors.
Core methods
In-season influenza vaccination was defined as receipt of an influenza vaccine between September and March of a given season. Generalized estimating equations (GEE) logistic regression with an exchangeable correlation structure estimated factors associated with vaccination uptake, adjusting for age, sex, nationality, governorate, profession, managerial position, previous-year vaccination status, and pre-season sickness absence duration.
Main results
Overall in-season vaccination uptake was 8.7%, with repeated vaccination across seasons being uncommon. Older age, male sex, non-Kuwaiti nationality, and being a physician were independently associated with higher odds of vaccination. Previous-year vaccination was the strongest predictor (aOR 2.01, 95% CI 1.87–2.16; p < 0.001). Pre-season sickness absence showed a non-linear association: 1–3 days slightly increased odds (aOR 1.08, 95% CI 1.03–1.14), while ≥8 days decreased odds (aOR 0.91, 95% CI 0.85–0.97).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the low uptake and limited continuity as evidence of persistent barriers to vaccination among HP in Kuwait. They highlighted the dominant role of previous-year vaccination as a predictor, suggesting that interventions should prioritize sustaining annual uptake rather than initiating first-time vaccination.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings indicate that targeted strategies are needed to improve in-season influenza vaccination among HP in Kuwait, particularly focusing on groups with lower uptake such as younger staff and those with prolonged pre-season sickness absence. The study also underscores the importance of maintaining vaccination records to support continuity of care.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study relied on administrative records, which may lack granular data on individual attitudes, beliefs, or access barriers. The observational design limits causal inference, and generalizability to other settings or populations remains uncertain. The evidence strength is assessed as moderate due to these limitations.

安徽利辛县2024–2025年住院儿童六种呼吸道病原体的临床与流行病学特征

Clinical and epidemiological profiles of six respiratory pathogens in hospitalized children in Lixin County, Anhui, China (2024–2025)
作者： Tong Xu, 吴小磊, Fang Lin, Xiaolei Wu
摘要中文翻译背景 急性呼吸道感染由多种病原体引起。本研究旨在通过分析住院儿童患者的病原体检出率,调查主要呼吸道病原体的流行病学特征与临床特点。方法 本研究回顾性纳入利辛县人民医院儿科诊断为急性呼吸道感染的住院患儿的鼻咽拭子标本,时间为2024年2月至2025年8月。所有标本均采用聚合酶链反应(PCR)检测六种主要呼吸道病原体,包括腺病毒、呼吸道合胞病毒、甲型流感病毒、乙型流感病毒、副流感病毒及肺炎支原体。基于PCR结果,进一步分析各病原体的流行病学趋势、年龄分层分布及对应临床特征。结果 六种呼吸道病原体的流行病学与临床特征总结如下。总体阳性率呈冬春季节高发,其中腺病毒与呼吸道合胞病毒的流行窗口最长(分别为11月至次年7月、11月至次年5月),甲型流感病毒则相对局限(12月至次年3月)。肺炎支原体呈散发年度模式,无统计学意义的季节性高峰。年龄分布差异显著:呼吸道合胞病毒主要感染1岁儿童,甲型流感病毒则略偏向婴幼儿。临床上,发热与咳嗽为普遍症状,而呼吸道合胞病毒的喘息发生率显著升高(27.57%,其他病原体为3.97%–9.52%)。热性惊厥最常见于甲型流感病毒感染(6.29%),其次为腺病毒(3.57%)。呼吸困难或发绀在所有病原体中均罕见(≤0.45%)。结论 冬季多种呼吸道病原体活跃流行。不同病原体引起的急性呼吸道感染在年龄分布与临床表现上存在差异。结合当地流行病学数据,这些特征有助于临床医师快速识别可能的病原体。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
急性呼吸道感染由多种病原体引起。本研究旨在通过分析住院儿童患者的病原体检出率,调查主要呼吸道病原体的流行病学特征与临床特点。
设计与对象
本研究回顾性纳入利辛县人民医院儿科诊断为急性呼吸道感染的住院患儿的鼻咽拭子标本,时间为2024年2月至2025年8月。研究对象为2024–2025年安徽省利辛县的住院儿童。
核心方法
所有标本均采用聚合酶链反应(PCR)检测六种主要呼吸道病原体,包括腺病毒、呼吸道合胞病毒、甲型流感病毒、乙型流感病毒、副流感病毒及肺炎支原体。基于PCR结果,进一步分析各病原体的流行病学趋势、年龄分层分布及对应临床特征。
主要结果
六种呼吸道病原体的流行病学与临床特征总结如下。总体阳性率呈冬春季节高发,其中腺病毒与呼吸道合胞病毒的流行窗口最长(分别为11月至次年7月、11月至次年5月),甲型流感病毒则相对局限(12月至次年3月)。肺炎支原体呈散发年度模式,无统计学意义的季节性高峰。年龄分布差异显著:呼吸道合胞病毒主要感染1岁儿童,甲型流感病毒则略偏向婴幼儿。临床上,发热与咳嗽为普遍症状,而呼吸道合胞病毒的喘息发生率显著升高(27.57%,其他病原体为3.97%–9.52%)。
解释与创新
不同病原体引起的急性呼吸道感染在年龄分布与临床表现上存在差异。结合当地流行病学数据,这些特征有助于临床医师快速识别可能的病原体。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可结合当地流行病学数据,帮助临床医生迅速识别可能的致病病原体。这些特征可以为 监测 和 诊断 儿童呼吸道感染策略提供参考。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据未报告具体研究局限。证据基于PCR检测与回顾性数据,可能引入一定偏倚与解释上的局限性。

Clinical and epidemiological profiles of six respiratory pathogens in hospitalized children in Lixin County, Anhui, China (2024–2025)

Authors: Tong Xu, 吴小磊, Fang Lin, Xiaolei Wu
Original abstract (English)
Background Acute respiratory infections are caused by a diverse range of pathogens. This study aimed to investigate the epidemiological profiles and clinical features of major respiratory pathogens by analyzing their detection rates in hospitalized pediatric patients. Methods Nasopharyngeal swab specimens were retrospectively enrolled from pediatric inpatients diagnosed with acute respiratory infections at the Department of Pediatrics, Lixin County People's Hospital, between February 2024 and August 2025. All specimens were subjected to polymerase chain reaction (PCR) assays targeting six major respiratory pathogens, namely adenovirus, respiratory syncytial virus, influenza A virus, influenza B virus, parainfluenza virus, and Mycoplasma pneumoniae . Based on the PCR results, we further delineated the epidemiological trends, age-stratified distribution, and corresponding clinical profiles associated with each identified pathogen. Results The epidemiological and clinical profiles of the six detected respiratory pathogens are summarized below. In brief, the overall positivity rate of pathogens exhibited winter-spring predominance, with adenovirus and RSV showing the broadest outbreak windows (November–July and November–May, respectively), while influenza A was more restricted (December–March). Mycoplasma pneumoniae displayed a sporadic annual pattern without a statistically significant seasonal peak. Age distribution varied substantially: RSV targeted children 1 year, and influenza A showed a slight infant-toddler predominance. Clinically, while fever and cough were universal, RSV was distinguished by a significantly elevated wheezing rate (27.57% vs. 3.97%–9.52% for others), Febrile seizures were most frequently observed in influenza A virus infections (6.29%), followed by adenovirus (3.57%). Dyspnea or cyanosis was uniformly rare across all pathogens (≤ 0.45%). Conclusion During the winter season, multiple respiratory pathogens tend to circulate actively. Acute respiratory infections caused by different pathogens exhibit distinct age distributions and clinical manifestations. These features, when combined with local epidemiological data, may assist clinicians in promptly identifying the likely causative pathogen.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Acute respiratory infections are caused by a diverse range of pathogens. This study aimed to investigate the epidemiological profiles and clinical features of major respiratory pathogens by analyzing their detection rates in hospitalized pediatric patients.
Design and population
Nasopharyngeal swab specimens were retrospectively enrolled from pediatric inpatients diagnosed with acute respiratory infections at the Department of Pediatrics, Lixin County People's Hospital, between February 2024 and August 2025. The study focused on hospitalized children in Lixin County, Anhui, China, during the period of 2024–2025.
Core methods
All specimens were subjected to polymerase chain reaction (PCR) assays targeting six major respiratory pathogens, namely adenovirus, respiratory syncytial virus, influenza A virus, influenza B virus, parainfluenza virus, and Mycoplasma pneumoniae. Based on the PCR results, we further delineated the epidemiological trends, age-stratified distribution, and corresponding clinical profiles associated with each identified pathogen.
Main results
The epidemiological and clinical profiles of the six detected respiratory pathogens are summarized below. In brief, the overall positivity rate of pathogens exhibited winter-spring predominance, with adenovirus and RSV showing the broadest outbreak windows (November–July and November–May, respectively), while influenza A was more restricted (December–March). Mycoplasma pneumoniae displayed a sporadic annual pattern without a statistically significant seasonal peak. Age distribution varied substantially: RSV targeted children 1 year, and influenza A showed a slight infant-toddler predominance. Clinically, while fever and cough were universal, RSV was distinguished by a significantly elevated wheezing rate (27.57% vs. 3.97%–9.52% for others).
Interpretation and novelty
Acute respiratory infections caused by different pathogens exhibit distinct age distributions and clinical manifestations. These features, when combined with local epidemiological data, may assist clinicians in promptly identifying the likely causative pathogen.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may assist clinicians in promptly identifying the likely causative pathogen by combining features with local epidemiological data. These features can inform surveillance and diagnosis strategies for respiratory infections in pediatric populations.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations of the study. The evidence is based on PCR assays and retrospective data, which may introduce some biases and limitations in interpretation.

亚型特异性流感在极端天气事件下的风险估计:以高湿度为主要预测因子在亚热带沿海城市的预测意义

Subtype-specific risk estimates of influenza under extreme weather events in a subtropical coastal city: high humidity as a major factor associated with forecasting implications
作者： Hansong Zhu, Can Li, Han Xu, Jinming Zheng, Yuyan Chen, Xiaoyuan Zhang, Jinxin You, Zhonghang Xie, Xiaoqi Liu, Xiucheng Xiao
摘要中文翻译气象条件是流感传播的重要环境决定因素;然而,极端天气事件(EWEs)对健康的影响仍未得到充分表征,尤其是在亚热带沿海地区。本研究以中国厦门为案例,系统评估了EWEs对流感的异质性关联,并量化其独立预测贡献。采用分布滞后非线性模型(DLNM)分析2013–2024年每日监测数据,评估11类EWEs对不同流感亚型和人群分层的风险,并调整了空气污染物和人口流动性。随机森林(RF)模型进一步评估了预测贡献。显著的EWEs特异性效应显现:极端高湿度事件(EHRHUE)与流感B(Yamagata)亚型关联最强(21天累积相对风险[CRR] = 2.51),而极端低温事件(ELTEME)与甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09关联最强(21天CRR = 1.72)。极端低风速事件(ELWINE)和极端长日照时长事件(EHSSDE)分别对B(Yamagata)亚型(21天CRR = 3.11)和甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09(21天CRR = 3.36)显示出较高风险。特征重要性分析显示EHRHUE为主导预测因子(贡献度35.59%),其次为PM10(18.64%)。将EWEs纳入RF模型后,性能提升:Nash–Sutcliffe效率(NSE)提高26.82%,其他指标亦有改善。本研究证实EWEs与亚热带沿海城市流感传播显著且异质性关联,其中EHRHUE、PM10和温度为关键环境预测因子。将EWEs纳入预测模型可提升流感预测准确性,并为气候适应性早期预警系统和精准公共卫生干预提供科学依据。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
气象条件是流感传播的重要环境决定因素;然而,极端天气事件(EWEs)对健康的影响仍未得到充分表征,尤其是在亚热带沿海地区。
设计与对象
本研究以中国厦门为案例,系统评估了EWEs对流感的异质性关联,并量化其独立预测贡献。
核心方法
采用分布滞后非线性模型(DLNM)分析2013–2024年每日监测数据,评估11类EWEs对不同流感亚型和人群分层的风险,并调整了空气污染物和人口流动性。
主要结果
显著的EWEs特异性效应显现:极端高湿度事件(EHRHUE)与流感B(Yamagata)亚型关联最强(21天累积相对风险[CRR] = 2.51),而极端低温事件(ELTEME)与甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09关联最强(21天CRR = 1.72)。
解释与创新
本研究证实EWEs与亚热带沿海城市流感传播显著且异质性关联,其中EHRHUE、PM10和温度为关键环境预测因子。
科研与公卫意义
将EWEs纳入预测模型可提升流感预测准确性,并为气候适应性早期预警系统和精准公共卫生干预提供科学依据。
局限与证据强度
特征重要性分析显示EHRHUE为主导预测因子(贡献度35.59%),其次为PM10(18.64%)。

Subtype-specific risk estimates of influenza under extreme weather events in a subtropical coastal city: high humidity as a major factor associated with forecasting implications

Authors: Hansong Zhu, Can Li, Han Xu, Jinming Zheng, Yuyan Chen, Xiaoyuan Zhang, Jinxin You, Zhonghang Xie, Xiaoqi Liu, Xiucheng Xiao
Original abstract (English)
Meteorological conditions are important environmental determinants of influenza transmission; however, the health impacts of extreme weather events (EWEs) remain insufficiently characterized, particularly in subtropical coastal regions. Using Xiamen, China, as a case study, we systematically evaluated the heterogeneous associations between EWEs on influenza and quantified their independent predictive contributions. Daily surveillance data (2013–2024) were analyzed using distributed lag nonlinear models (DLNM) to assess risks of 11 EWE categories across influenza subtypes and population strata, adjusting for air pollutants and population mobility. Random forest (RF) modeling further evaluated predictive contributions. Pronounced EWE-specific effects emerged: extreme high humidity event (EHRHUE) most strongly associated with influenza B(Yamagata) (21-day cumulative relative risk [CRR] = 2.51), while extreme low temperature event (ELTEME) most strongly associated with A(H3N2) (21-day CRR = 1.72). Extreme low wind speed event (ELWINE) and extreme long sunshine duration event (EHSSDE) showed elevated risks for B(Yamagata) (21-day CRR = 3.11) and A(H3N2) (21-day CRR = 3.36), respectively. Feature importance identified EHRHUE as the dominant predictor (35.59% contribution), followed by PM10 (18.64%). Incorporating EWEs into RF improved performance: Nash–Sutcliffe efficiency (NSE) increased 26.82%; other metrics also enhanced. This study confirms that EWEs were significantly and heterogeneously associated with influenza transmission in subtropical coastal cities, with EHRHUE, PM10, and temperature as key environmental predictors. Integrating EWEs into predictive models enhances influenza forecasting accuracy and provides a scientific foundation for climate-adaptive early warning systems and precision public health interventions.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Meteorological conditions are important environmental determinants of influenza transmission; however, the health impacts of extreme weather events (EWEs) remain insufficiently characterized, particularly in subtropical coastal regions.
Design and population
Using Xiamen, China, as a case study, we systematically evaluated the heterogeneous associations between EWEs on influenza and quantified their independent predictive contributions.
Core methods

Main results

系统性去除宿主限制因子可实现细胞培养基础上疫苗的快速、可扩展生产

Systemic removal of host restriction factors enables rapid, scalable virus production for cell culture-based vaccine development
作者： Zhengmei Xu, Jaemyeong Jeon, Jinsoo Oh, Youngki Shin, Se‐Ho Park
摘要中文翻译细胞培养基础上疫苗的生产效率受到宿主细胞抗病毒药物防御机制的根本性限制,为快速与大规模疫苗制造带来重大瓶颈。关键抗病毒药物蛋白(如RNase L、PKR与JAK1)作为病毒复制的内在抑制因子,限制了多种临床相关病毒的有效传播。为克服此挑战,研究者构建了HEK293T、Vero与MDCK细胞系,并对多个抗病毒药物基因进行了定向敲除。值得注意的是,这些工程化细胞在维持正常生长与存活的同时,显著提升了病毒产量。多基因敲除可增强包括流感A病毒、假型慢病毒与猪流行性腹泻病毒(PEDV)等包膜病毒,以及柯萨奇病毒等无包膜病毒的复制。增强幅度与敲除基因数量呈正相关。通过系统性去除宿主限制因子,该平台为加速病毒扩增提供了一种通用且强大的策略,为更高效的细胞培养基础上抗病毒疫苗的研发与大规模生产奠定坚实基础。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在解决细胞培养基础上疫苗生产效率低下的挑战,该效率受限于宿主细胞抗病毒药物防御机制(如RNase L、PKR与JAK1)。这些防御机制作为病毒复制的内在抑制因子,为快速与大规模疫苗制造带来重大瓶颈。
设计与对象
研究构建了HEK293T、Vero与MDCK细胞系,并对多个抗病毒药物基因进行了定向敲除。这些工程化细胞用于支持多种病毒的复制,包括甲型流感病毒pdm09、甲型H3N2流感病毒,以及B/Victoria与B/Yamagata进化支。
核心方法
研究者通过定向敲除抗病毒药物基因构建细胞系,以去除宿主限制因子。该方法用于提升细胞培养系统中的病毒复制效率。
主要结果
工程化细胞在维持正常生长与存活的同时,显著提升了病毒产量。多基因敲除可增强包膜病毒与无包膜病毒的复制,且增强幅度与敲除基因数量呈正相关。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,系统性去除宿主限制因子可显著提升细胞培养中的病毒扩增效率,为加速疫苗研发提供新策略。该方法为更高效的细胞培养基础上抗病毒疫苗生产奠定坚实基础。
科研与公卫意义
该平台可为甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09等季节性流感病毒的细胞培养基础上疫苗研发提供参考,有望提升疫苗生产能力并缩短暴发响应时间。
局限与证据强度
本研究仅限于摘要层面的证据,结果基于工程化细胞系而非直接临床测试。由于缺乏详细实验验证与真实世界应用数据,证据强度为中等。

Systemic removal of host restriction factors enables rapid, scalable virus production for cell culture-based vaccine development

Authors: Zhengmei Xu, Jaemyeong Jeon, Jinsoo Oh, Youngki Shin, Se‐Ho Park
Original abstract (English)
The efficiency of cell culture-based vaccine production is fundamentally constrained by the antiviral defenses of host cells, creating a major bottleneck for rapid and large-scale vaccine manufacturing. Key antiviral proteins-such as RNase L, PKR, and JAK1 act as intrinsic brakes on viral replication, limiting efficient propagation of many clinically relevant viruses. To overcome this challenge, we generated HEK293T, Vero, and MDCK cell lines with targeted knockouts of multiple antiviral genes. Notably, these engineered cells maintained normal growth and viability while supporting markedly increased viral yields. Multi-gene deletions enhance the replication of both enveloped viruses, including influenza A virus, pseudotyped lentivirus, and porcine epidemic diarrhea virus (PEDV), and non-enveloped viruses such as coxsackievirus. The magnitude of enhancement scaled proportionally with the number of genes disrupted. By systematically removing host restriction factors, this platform provides a versatile and powerful strategy for accelerating viral propagation, offering a strong foundation for more efficient development and large-scale production of cell culture-based anti-viral vaccines.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the challenge of inefficient cell culture-based vaccine production, which is limited by host antiviral defenses such as RNase L, PKR, and JAK1. These defenses act as intrinsic brakes on viral replication, creating a major bottleneck for rapid and large-scale vaccine manufacturing.
Design and population
The study involved generating HEK293T, Vero, and MDCK cell lines with targeted knockouts of multiple antiviral genes. These engineered cells were used to support the replication of various viruses, including influenza A(H1N1)pdm09, A(H3N2), and B/Victoria and B/Yamagata lineages.
Core methods
The researchers created cell lines with targeted deletions of antiviral genes to remove host restriction factors. This approach was used to enhance viral replication in cell culture systems.
Main results
The engineered cells maintained normal growth and viability while significantly increasing viral yields. Multi-gene deletions enhanced the replication of both enveloped and non-enveloped viruses, with the magnitude of enhancement scaling proportionally with the number of genes disrupted.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate that systematic removal of host restriction factors can greatly improve viral propagation in cell culture, offering a new strategy for accelerating vaccine development. This approach provides a strong foundation for more efficient production of cell culture-based anti-viral vaccines.
Scientific and public-health significance
This platform can inform the development of more efficient cell culture-based vaccines for seasonal influenza viruses, potentially improving vaccine manufacturing capacity and response times during outbreaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to abstract-level evidence, and the results are based on engineered cell lines without direct clinical testing. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the lack of detailed experimental validation and real-world application data.

系统层面的洞察:年龄依赖性先天免疫应答在流感B病毒感染中的作用

Systems-level insights into age-dependent innate immune responses in influenza B infection
作者： Shikha Saxena, Gurleen Kaur, Preeti Vishwakarma, Varun Kumar, Amit Kumar, Sonia Ompal, Satish Kumar, Sarjeet Singh, Akanksha Chauhan, Susmita Chaudhuri
摘要中文翻译衰老已知会改变先天免疫功能,增加对病毒感染的易感性,但其对流感B病毒(IBV)感染的具体影响仍不明确。为解决这一问题,我们使用一种临床相关的IBV毒株Inf B/Phuket/3073/2013建立了一个临床前小鼠模型,比较年轻成年小鼠和老年小鼠的先天免疫应答,特别关注感染期间肺部与年龄相关的转录和蛋白质组学改变。对感染后采集的肺组织进行批量RNA测序和蛋白质组学分析,揭示了不同的年龄依赖性转录特征。批量RNA测序分析发现了1,350个差异表达基因(DEGs),其中824个表现出年龄偏向性上调,并在流感B病毒感染的老年小鼠肺部显著上调。这些DEGs的功能富集分析表明,老年小鼠的先天免疫信号传导、细胞凋亡以及与细胞应激和DNA损伤相关的通路存在扰动。在年轻成年和老年流感B病毒感染中,已鉴定出几种差异表达的蛋白质。其中一些共同共表达的蛋白质在病毒入侵、信号传导通路和干扰素反应中发挥作用。实时PCR分析证实了关键先天免疫基因的表达改变,包括Toll样受体(TLRs)、干扰素调节因子(IRF7和IRF9)、关键抗病毒药物基因(IFIT-2、IFIT-3和STAT-1)以及I型和II型干扰素(IFN-α、IFN-β、IFN-γ)。值得注意的是,年轻成年小鼠表现出更协调且更强的抗病毒药物通路激活,而老年小鼠则表现出延迟和异常的免疫应答,特征为过度炎症和干扰素信号传导受损。重要性:本研究提供了对流感B病毒(IBV)感染期间年龄驱动的先天免疫改变的全面系统层面分析。尽管已有证据表明衰老会损害多种病毒感染中的抗病毒药物反应,但我们的研究结果特别表明,老年小鼠感染IBV会导致先天免疫通路的不成比例和失调的转录激活,包括过度的I型干扰素信号传导和炎症介质谱的改变,并揭示了潜在的治疗靶点,以改善老年人群体的流感感染健康结局。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
衰老已知会改变先天免疫功能并增加对病毒感染的易感性,但其对流感B病毒(IBV)感染的具体影响仍不明确,这造成了一个知识空白,本研究旨在解决这一问题。
设计与对象
本研究使用一种临床相关的IBV毒株(Inf B/Phuket/3073/2013)建立临床前小鼠模型,比较年轻成年和老年小鼠的先天免疫应答,特别关注感染期间肺组织中与年龄相关的转录和蛋白质组学改变。
核心方法
对感染后采集的肺组织进行批量RNA测序和蛋白质组学分析,以鉴定差异表达的基因和蛋白质,并使用实时PCR确认关键先天免疫基因(包括TLRs、IRF7、IRF9、IFIT-2、IFIT-3、STAT-1和干扰素)的表达改变。
主要结果
批量RNA测序发现了1,350个差异表达基因,其中824个在感染肺部表现出年龄偏向性上调,功能富集分析表明老年小鼠的先天免疫信号传导、细胞凋亡以及与细胞应激和DNA损伤相关的通路存在扰动。
解释与创新
作者将研究结果解释为表明,老年小鼠感染IBV会导致先天免疫通路的不成比例和失调的转录激活,包括过度的I型干扰素信号传导和炎症介质谱的改变,突出了改善老年人流感感染健康结局的潜在治疗靶点。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,IBV感染期间年龄依赖性的先天免疫失调可能为针对增强抗病毒药物反应和减少老年群体过度炎症的干预措施提供依据。
局限与证据强度
本研究的局限性在于其使用的是临床前小鼠模型,可能无法完全再现人类的免疫应答,且由于缺乏人类验证以及模型特异性免疫动态的潜在干扰,证据强度中等。

Systems-level insights into age-dependent innate immune responses in influenza B infection

Authors: Shikha Saxena, Gurleen Kaur, Preeti Vishwakarma, Varun Kumar, Amit Kumar, Sonia Ompal, Satish Kumar, Sarjeet Singh, Akanksha Chauhan, Susmita Chaudhuri
Original abstract (English)
Aging is known to alter innate immune function, increasing susceptibility to viral infections, yet its specific effects on influenza B virus (IBV) infection remain poorly characterized. To address this gap, we developed a preclinical mouse model using a clinically relevant IBV strain Inf B/Phuket/3073/2013 to compare innate immune responses between young adult and aged mice, with a particular focus on age-related transcriptional and proteomic alterations in lung during infection. Bulk RNA sequencing and proteomics analysis of lung tissue harvested post-infection revealed distinct age-dependent transcriptional profiles. Bulk RNA-seq analysis identified 1,350 differentially expressed genes (DEGs), of which 824 exhibited an aged-biased expression pattern and were significantly upregulated in the lungs of influenza B virus-infected aged mice. Functional enrichment of these DEGs indicated perturbations in innate immune signaling, apoptosis, and pathways related to cellular stress and DNA damage in aged mice. There are several differentially expressed proteins that have been identified in young adult and aged influenza B infection. Among these differentially expressed proteins, some commonly co-expressed proteins have a role in virus entry, signaling pathways, and interferon response. Real-time PCR analysis confirmed altered expression of key innate immune genes, including Toll-like receptors (TLRs), interferon regulatory factors (IRF7 and IRF9), key antiviral genes (IFIT -2, IFIT-3 and STAT-1) and type I and II interferons (IFN-α, IFN-β, IFN-γ). Notably, young adult mice exhibited a more coordinated and robust activation of antiviral pathways, whereas aged mice demonstrated delayed and aberrant immune responses, characterized by excessive inflammation and impaired interferon signaling. IMPORTANCE: This study provides a comprehensive system-level analysis of age-driven alterations in innate immunity during influenza B virus (IBV) infection. While prior evidence has established that aging impairs antiviral responses across various viral infections, our findings specifically demonstrate that IBV infection in aged mice leads to a disproportionate and dysregulated transcriptional activation of innate immune pathways, including excessive type I interferon signaling and altered inflammatory mediator profiles, and uncover the potential therapeutic targets to improve health outcomes in the elderly population on influenza infection.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Aging is known to alter innate immune function and increase susceptibility to viral infections, yet the specific effects of aging on influenza B virus (IBV) infection remain poorly characterized, creating a knowledge gap that this study aimed to address.
Design and population
The study used a preclinical mouse model with a clinically relevant IBV strain (Inf B/Phuket/3073/2013) to compare innate immune responses between young adult and aged mice, focusing on age-related transcriptional and proteomic alterations in lung tissue during infection.
Core methods
Bulk RNA sequencing and proteomics were performed on lung tissue harvested post-infection to identify differentially expressed genes and proteins, and real-time PCR was used to confirm altered expression of key innate immune genes including TLRs, IRF7, IRF9, IFIT-2, IFIT-3, STAT-1, and interferons.
Main results
Bulk RNA-seq identified 1,350 differentially expressed genes, with 824 showing aged-biased upregulation in infected lungs, and functional enrichment indicated perturbations in innate immune signaling, apoptosis, and stress-related pathways in aged mice.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as demonstrating that IBV infection in aged mice leads to disproportionate and dysregulated transcriptional activation of innate immune pathways, including excessive type I interferon signaling and altered inflammatory mediator profiles, highlighting potential therapeutic targets for improving health outcomes in the elderly.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings suggest that age-dependent innate immune dysregulation during IBV infection may inform targeted interventions to enhance antiviral responses and reduce excessive inflammation in elderly populations.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its preclinical mouse model design, which may not fully recapitulate human immune responses, and the evidence strength is moderate due to the absence of human validation and potential confounding from model-specific immune dynamics.

基于CRISPR的‘决堤排水’传感架构:实现快速、经济且超灵敏的病原体检测

A CRISPR-based ‘dam-break drainage’ sensing architecture for rapid, affordable and ultrasensitive pathogen detection
作者： Kai Liu, Xiang Li, Meng Zhao, Zhuo Yang, J.M. Wen, Jinpeng Liu, Haoli Zhang, Yuecan Zhao, Yang Sun, Zhengping Li
摘要中文翻译摘要 核酸检测对传染病诊断至关重要,但如何在保证高灵敏度的同时实现可及性检测仍具挑战。本研究展示了一种芯片化视觉检测策略,可针对一种或两种病毒RNA靶标进行检测,仅需加样即可完成,无需进一步仪器操作或人工驱动。靶标激活的CRISPR-Cas13a改变分子屏障的润湿性,使液体突破预设阈值并产生决堤排水信号。该芯片可在2分钟内检测单个靶标至10 aM,或在5分钟内检测两个靶标至100 aM,单次检测成本分别为0.20美元和0.30美元。我们进一步建立了实用的临床样本工作流程,结合室温裂解与RNase抑制,并在40份鼻拭子样本中验证了该平台对SARS-CoV-2、甲型流感病毒和乙型流感病毒的检测,结果与RT-PCR一致。该阈值导向的流体策略为在非中心化实验室环境下实现快速、低成本且无仪器依赖的分子检测提供了途径。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
核酸检测对传染病诊断至关重要,但如何在保证高灵敏度的同时实现可及性检测仍具挑战。本研究旨在开发一种快速、经济且超灵敏的病原体检测方法。
设计与对象
本研究提出一种芯片化视觉检测策略,可针对一种或两种病毒RNA靶标进行检测,仅需加样即可完成,无需进一步仪器操作或人工驱动。该平台在40份鼻拭子样本中进行验证,检测对象包括SARS-CoV-2、甲型流感病毒和乙型流感病毒。
核心方法
本研究采用实用的临床样本工作流程,结合室温裂解与RNase抑制,并在40份鼻拭子样本中验证了该平台对SARS-CoV-2、甲型流感病毒和乙型流感病毒的检测,结果与RT-PCR一致。
主要结果
该芯片可在2分钟内检测单个靶标至10 aM,或在5分钟内检测两个靶标至100 aM,单次检测成本分别为0.20美元和0.30美元。
解释与创新
该阈值导向的流体策略为在非中心化实验室环境下实现快速、低成本且无仪器依赖的分子检测提供了途径。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果为改善资源受限环境下的病原体检测提供了有前景的方案,有助于提升对流感病毒等人兽共患病病毒的监测与诊断能力。
局限与证据强度
本研究仅基于摘要层面的证据,且样本量较小(40份鼻拭子样本)。证据强度为中等,因研究未提供详细统计分析或长期随访结果。

A CRISPR-based ‘dam-break drainage’ sensing architecture for rapid, affordable and ultrasensitive pathogen detection

Authors: Kai Liu, Xiang Li, Meng Zhao, Zhuo Yang, J.M. Wen, Jinpeng Liu, Haoli Zhang, Yuecan Zhao, Yang Sun, Zhengping Li
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Nucleic acid testing is important for infectious-disease diagnosis, but combining high sensitivity with accessible testing remains challenging. Here we show a chip-based visual detection strategy for one or two viral RNA targets that involves only sample loading, without further instrumentation or manual actuation. Target-activated CRISPR-Cas13a alters the wettability of a molecular barrier, causing liquid to cross a preset threshold and generate a dam-break drainage signal. The chip detects a single target at 10 aM within 2 min and two targets at 100 aM within 5 min, with costs of $0.20 and $0.30 per test, respectively. We further establish a practical clinical-sample workflow combining room-temperature lysis with RNase inhibition and validate the platform across 40 nasal-swab samples tested for SARS-CoV-2, influenza A virus and influenza B virus, with results concordant with RT–PCR. This threshold-based fluidic strategy provides a route towards rapid, low-cost and instrument-free molecular testing outside centralized laboratories.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Nucleic acid testing is important for infectious-disease diagnosis, but combining high sensitivity with accessible testing remains challenging. The study aimed to develop a rapid, affordable and ultrasensitive pathogen detection method.
Design and population
The study presents a chip-based visual detection strategy for one or two viral RNA targets, involving only sample loading without further instrumentation or manual actuation. It validates the platform across 40 nasal-swab samples tested for SARS-CoV-2, influenza A virus and influenza B virus.
Core methods
The methods include a practical clinical-sample workflow combining room-temperature lysis with RNase inhibition and validate the platform across 40 nasal-swab samples tested for SARS-CoV-2, influenza A virus and influenza B virus, with results concordant with RT–PCR.
Main results
The chip detects a single target at 10 aM within 2 min and two targets at 100 aM within 5 min, with costs of $0.20 and $0.30 per test, respectively.
Interpretation and novelty
The threshold-based fluidic strategy provides a route towards rapid, low-cost and instrument-free molecular testing outside centralized laboratories.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings offer a promising approach for improving pathogen detection in resource-limited settings, which could enhance surveillance and diagnosis of seasonal influenza viruses.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to abstract-only evidence, and the results are based on a small sample size of 40 nasal-swab samples. The evidence strength is moderate as the study does not provide detailed statistical analyses or long-term follow-up.

MoActiPred:一种用于流感神经氨酸酶抑制剂开发的基于人工智能的生物活性预测模型

MoActiPred: an AI-Based bioactivity prediction model applied to the development of influenza neuraminidase inhibitors
作者： Zeqi Zhang, Jiqing Ye, Qingyu Zou, Chenyi Ji, Stephanie Holt, Patrice Guillon, Larissa Dirr, Long Yin Lam, Tsz Fung Tsang, Xiao Yang
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
准确预测小分子的生物活性对于加速药物发现至关重要。本研究旨在开发一种基于人工智能的模型,用于预测流感神经氨酸酶抑制剂的生物活性。
设计与对象
本研究涉及使用一个经过整理的源自ChEMBL的流感病毒神经氨酸酶抑制剂数据集,开发和评估MoActiPred这一多模态人工智能框架。该模型的前瞻性应用价值通过一系列设计、实验合成并针对甲型H3N2流感病毒神经氨酸酶(NA)进行评估的奥司他韦类似物进行测试。
核心方法
MoActiPred使用包含1245个化合物的数据集(IC50阈值为20 nM)进行了训练和验证。该模型集成了2D分子图、理化描述符和3D E(3)-等变几何表示。其前瞻性应用通过自主合成奥司他韦类似物并随后进行酶学和细胞水平生物活性测试进行了评估。
主要结果
On the public test set, MoActiPred achieved an accuracy of 0.952, an MCC of 0.797, and an F1 score of 0.898. The model correctly predicted 6 of 7 high-activity compounds (IC50 100 μM).
解释与创新
The results demonstrate that MoActiPred outperforms conventional machine-learning models, SMILES-based language models, and graph-based baselines in classification performance. This highlights the value of combining 2D and 3D molecular representations for bioactivity prediction.
科研与公卫意义
这些发现支持在流感病毒药物发现研究中使用MoActiPred,特别是在识别如5b这样的有前景的先导化合物方面。
局限与证据强度
本研究仅限于奥司他韦类似物的体外测试,未包括临床试验或更广泛的宿主范围验证。证据强度较高,因为该模型在整理的数据集和公共数据集上均进行了评估,表现出一致的性能。

MoActiPred: an AI-Based bioactivity prediction model applied to the development of influenza neuraminidase inhibitors

Authors: Zeqi Zhang, Jiqing Ye, Qingyu Zou, Chenyi Ji, Stephanie Holt, Patrice Guillon, Larissa Dirr, Long Yin Lam, Tsz Fung Tsang, Xiao Yang
Original abstract (English)
Accurate prediction of small-molecule bioactivity is critical for accelerating drug discovery. Here, we report MoActiPred, a multimodal AI framework that integrates 2D molecular graphs, physicochemical descriptors, and 3D E (3)-equivariant geometric representations. Using a curated ChEMBL-derived influenza virus neuraminidase (NA) inhibitor dataset (1245 compounds; IC 50 threshold 20 nM), MoActiPred showed the strongest overall performance among the evaluated methods, including traditional machine-learning models (Random Forest, SVM, and XGBoost), SMILES-based language models (ChemBERTa, ChemFM, and PolyBERT), and graph-based baselines (MolCLR and SEGNN). On the public test set, MoActiPred obtained an accuracy of 0.952, MCC of 0.797, and F1 score of 0.898, while retaining high average precision, supporting the value of combining 2D and 3D representations. To evaluate its practical utility, MoActiPred was applied prospectively to 26 oseltamivir analogues designed, experimentally synthesised and evaluated against influenza A (H3N2) NA. The model correctly predicted 6 of 7 high-activity compounds (IC 50 100 μM). Docking, MD simulations, and water-map analyses indicated that the oseltamivir core of 5b occupies the conserved active site while its N -substituent engages the 150- and 430-cavities, with solvation-driven binding particularly favouring N2. These results support that MoActiPred’s utility in drug discovery research and identify 5b as a promising lead for further optimisation against influenza A (H3N2). Scientific contribution MoActiPred advances bioactivity prediction by integrating 2D molecular graphs, physicochemical descriptors, and 3D E (3)-equivariant geometric representations within a single multimodal framework. Compared with conventional machine-learning models, SMILES-based language models, and graph-based baselines, MoActiPred achieved stronger classification performance on the curated and public neuraminidase inhibitor datasets, demonstrating the value of combined 2D/3D molecular representation learning. Its prospective utility was further supported by in-house synthesis of a focused oseltamivir-analogue series followed by enzymatic and cell-based biological activity testing.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Accurate prediction of small-molecule bioactivity is critical for accelerating drug discovery. The study aimed to develop an AI-based model for predicting the bioactivity of influenza neuraminidase inhibitors.
Design and population
The study involved the development and evaluation of MoActiPred, a multimodal AI framework, using a curated ChEMBL-derived influenza virus neuraminidase inhibitor dataset. The prospective utility of the model was tested on a series of oseltamivir analogues synthesized and evaluated against influenza A (H3N2) NA.
Core methods
MoActiPred was trained and validated using a dataset of 1245 compounds with an IC50 threshold of 20 nM. The model integrated 2D molecular graphs, physicochemical descriptors, and 3D E(3)-equivariant geometric representations. Its prospective utility was assessed through in-house synthesis of oseltamivir analogues and subsequent enzymatic and cell-based biological activity testing.
Main results
On the public test set, MoActiPred achieved an accuracy of 0.952, an MCC of 0.797, and an F1 score of 0.898. The model correctly predicted 6 of 7 high-activity compounds (IC50 100 μM).
Interpretation and novelty
The results demonstrate that MoActiPred outperforms conventional machine-learning models, SMILES-based language models, and graph-based baselines in classification performance. This highlights the value of combining 2D and 3D molecular representations for bioactivity prediction.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings support the use of MoActiPred in drug discovery research for influenza viruses, particularly in identifying promising leads like 5b for further optimization against influenza A (H3N2).
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vitro testing of oseltamivir analogues and does not include clinical trials or broader host range validation. The evidence is strong, as the model was evaluated on both curated and public datasets, showing consistent performance.

青年患者流感相关ARDS管理:从诊断到治疗的多学科方法

Genç Hastada İnfluenza ile İlişkili ARDS Yönetimi: Tanıdan Tedaviye Multidisipliner Yaklaşım
作者： Mustafa Tosun
摘要中文翻译流感感染通常表现为自限性上呼吸道感染,但罕见情况下可导致严重病毒性肺炎和急性呼吸窘迫综合征(ARDS)。本病例报告中,一名无基础合并症的20岁男性患者因流感A感染继发ARDS,并在数小时内进展至重症,其诊疗及重症监护管理过程如下。患者初次就诊时仅表现为上呼吸道感染症状,未发现明显病理改变;约12小时后因严重呼吸困难和低氧血症再次就诊,胸部CT显示双侧广泛磨玻璃影及实变区域。因继发于病毒性肺炎的重症ARDS,患者择期行气管插管并收入重症监护病房,采用肺保护性机械通气策略。呼吸道标本PCR检测确认流感病毒甲型H3N2流感病毒感染。重症监护期间,患者氧合指数及影像学表现迅速改善,于入院第5天拔管,第7天转出至普通病房。本病例提示,流感感染可在年轻免疫功能正常个体中迅速进展为重症肺炎及ARDS,强调早期诊断与适当重症监护管理的重要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
流感感染通常表现为自限性上呼吸道感染,但罕见情况下可导致严重肺炎和急性呼吸窘迫综合征(ARDS)。此类情况在年轻免疫功能正常个体中可能引发严重并发症。
设计与对象
本病例报告描述了一名20岁免疫功能正常男性患者因流感A感染继发ARDS,并在数小时内进展至需重症监护的病例。患者初诊时未发现明显病理改变。
核心方法
通过PCR检测患者呼吸道标本确认流感病毒甲型H3N2流感病毒感染。同时评估患者临床症状及影像学表现。
主要结果
患者收入重症监护病房并采用肺保护性机械通气策略。重症监护期间,患者氧合指数及影像学表现迅速改善。
解释与创新
本病例表明,流感感染可在年轻免疫功能正常个体中迅速进展为重症肺炎及ARDS。强调早期诊断与适当重症监护管理的关键作用。
科研与公卫意义
该发现提示,流感感染在年轻免疫功能正常个体中可能引发严重并发症,凸显ARDS诊断中早期识别与重症监护策略的关键意义。
局限与证据强度
本病例报告基于单一患者,其可推广性有限。然而,该病例为流感病毒甲型H3N2流感病毒与ARDS之间关系提供了重要观察依据。

Genç Hastada İnfluenza ile İlişkili ARDS Yönetimi: Tanıdan Tedaviye Multidisipliner Yaklaşım

Authors: Mustafa Tosun
Original abstract (English)
İnfluenza enfeksiyonu çoğunlukla kendini sınırlayan üst solunum yolu enfeksiyonu şeklinde seyretmekle birlikte, nadiren ağır viral pnömoniye ve akut respiratuvar distres sendromuna (ARDS) yol açabilmektedir. Bu olgu sunumunda, altta yatan komorbiditesi bulunmayan 20 yaşındaki erkek hastada influenza A enfeksiyonuna sekonder, saatler içerisinde gelişen ağır ARDS tablosu ve yoğun bakım yönetimi sunulmuştur. Üst solunum yolu enfeksiyonu semptomları ile ilk başvurusunda belirgin patoloji saptanmayan hasta, yaklaşık 12 saat sonra ciddi dispne ve hipoksemi ile tekrar başvurmuş, acilde çekilen toraks BT’de bilateral yaygın buzlu cam ve konsolidasyon alanları izlenmiştir. Viral pnömoniye sekonder gelişen ağır ARDS nedeniyle hasta elektif olarak entübe edilerek yoğun bakım ünitesine alınmış ve akciğer koruyucu mekanik ventilasyon stratejileri uygulanmıştır. Solunum yolu örneklerinden yapılan polimeraz zincir reaksiyonu (PCR) testinde İnfluenza A(H3N2) saptanmıştır. Yoğun bakım izlemi sırasında oksijenizasyonu ve radyolojik bulguları hızla düzelen hasta yatışının beşinci gününde ekstübe edilmiş ve yedinci gününde servise devredilmiştir. Bu olgu, influenza enfeksiyonunun genç ve immünkompetan bireylerde hızlı ilerleyen ağır pnömoni ve ARDS tablosuna neden olabileceğini ve erken tanı ile uygun yoğun bakım yönetiminin önemini göstermektedir.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
İnfluenza enfeksiyonu, genellikle üst solunum yolu enfeksiyonu olarak sınırlanır; ancak nadiren ağır pnömoni ve akut respiratuvar distres sendromuna (ARDS) yol açabilir. Bu durum, özellikle genç ve immünkompetan bireylerde ciddi komplikasyonlara neden olabilir.
Design and population
Bu olgu sunumu, 20 yaşındaki immünkompetan bir erkekte influenza A enfeksiyonuna sekonder gelişen, saatler içerisinde ilerleyen ve yoğun bakım gerektiren ARDS tablosunu tanımlamaktadır. Hastanın başlangıçta belirgin patoloji saptanmamıştır.
Core methods
Hastanın solunum yolu örneklerinden yapılan PCR testi ile influenza A(H3N2) tespit edilmiştir. Ayrıca, hastanın klinik semptomları ve radyolojik bulguları değerlendirilmiştir.
Main results
Hasta, yoğun bakım ünitesine alınmış ve akciğer koruyucu mekanik ventilasyon stratejileri uygulanmıştır. Yoğun bakım izlemi sırasında oksijenizasyon ve radyolojik bulgular hızla düzelmeye başlamıştır.
Interpretation and novelty
Bu olgu, influenza enfeksiyonunun genç ve immünkompetan bireylerde hızlı ilerleyen ağır pnömoni ve ARDS tablosuna neden olabileceğini göstermektedir. Bu durum, erken tanı ve uygun yoğun bakım yönetiminin kritik önemini vurgulamaktadır.
Scientific and public-health significance
Bu bulgular, genç ve immünkompetan bireylerde influenza enfeksiyonunun ciddi komplikasyonlara yol açabileceği konusunda dikkat çeker. Bu, ARDS tanısında erken tanı ve yoğun bakım stratejilerinin kritik önemini ortaya koymaktadır.
Limitations and evidence strength
Bu olgu sunumu, tek bir hasta üzerine yapılmış olup, genelize edilebilirlik sınırlıdır. Ancak, influenza A(H3N2) ile ARDS arasındaki ilişkiye dair önemli bir gözleme dayanmaktadır.

2026年国际足联世界杯TM期间在团队集合场所进行空气采样以早期检测呼吸道病毒威胁

Air sampling in team congregate spaces for early detection of respiratory virus threats at the 2026 FIFA World CupTM
作者： Simon, D., Locksmith, T. J., Minor, N. R., Emmen, I. E., Wilson, N. A., OConnor, E. J., OConnor, S. L., OConnor, D. H.
摘要中文翻译呼吸道感染是大型体育赛事中疾病的主要原因,但监测依赖运动员识别和报告症状。我们评估了连续空气采样与即时分子检测是否能在精英团队集中空间的比赛期间检测到呼吸道病毒核酸,以及由此产生的信号是否具有操作价值。方法。我们对加拿大男子国家足球队在2026年国际足联世界杯期间(2026年6月3日至7月4日)横跨五个宿主城市进行前瞻性描述性环境-监测研究。InBio Apollo生物气溶胶采样器持续在每家酒店最多四个团队指定房间(理疗室、餐厅、设备室和教练室或走廊)内运行。滤膜每天更换约两次,现场洗脱并使用Cepheid Xpert® Xpress SARS-CoV-2/流感/RSV plus试剂盒检测。若任何靶标的循环阈值(Ct)值小于45,则样本判定为阳性。结果。在174个空气滤膜中,检测到13次病毒遗传物质(9次SARS-CoV-2,3次甲型流感病毒,1次乙型流感病毒,0次RSV)。检测在赛事早期稀少,晚期集中。流感A信号出现在一名发热球员被送回家的当天早晨,而SARS-CoV-2信号与酒店工作人员出现明显症状同时出现,在患病工作人员被排除后信号消失。结论。在精英团队的移动环境中,空气采样与即时检测可行,可在比赛期间提供不依赖行为的病毒信号,可能为更早采取预防措施提供机会。摘要框已知内容本话题已知内容呼吸道感染是大型体育赛事中疾病的主要原因,传统监测依赖运动员识别和报告症状,但会错过症状前期、轻症和无症状感染。空气采样可在集中场所检测呼吸道病毒,但未在比赛期间的精英团队住宿环境中评估。本研究的新增内容在为期五城市世界杯赛事期间,连续空气采样与当日即时检测可行,即使在北半球夏季低流行期,也能在团队共享空间中检测到SARS-CoV-2、甲型流感和乙型流感,部分信号与发热球员及明显症状工作人员同时出现。本研究可能对研究、实践或政策的影响空气采样提供了一种不依赖行为的早期预警方法,可让团队选择性升级预防措施,最大限度减少运动员和团队工作人员的病毒暴露。中心图表O_FIG O_LINKSMALLFIG WIDTH=200 HEIGHT=157 SRC="FIGDIR/small/26360542v1_ufig1.gif" ALT="图1"> 查看大图(44K):org.highwire.dtl.DTLVardef@1bc3ae7org.highwire.dtl.DTLVardef@bf47dforg.highwire.dtl.DTLVardef@7965forg.highwire.dtl.DTLVardef@214a7a_HPS_FORMAT_FIGEXP M_FIG 比赛期间精英团队呼吸道病毒威胁的空气采样早期检测。在2026年国际足联世界杯期间,于五个宿主城市的酒店内最多四个团队集中空间(餐厅、设备室、理疗室和教练室或走廊)持续进行生物气溶胶采样。每个采样器的滤膜在现场洗脱,并使用Cepheid GeneXpert SARS-CoV-2/流感/RSV plus试剂盒进行当日即时检测(上图)。在五个城市的团队酒店空气中均检测到病毒遗传物质。时间线以横向条带表示采样覆盖范围,以垂直标记表示整个赛事单一连续时间轴上的每次病毒检测,检测在早期稀少而在后期城市集中(中图)。阳性空气信号可在暴发临床表现前触发低成本、低风险响应措施,如佩戴口罩、便携式空气净化器、保持距离与减少入住率、远-UVC照明、加强通风及排除明显症状人员(下图)。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
呼吸道感染是大型体育赛事中最主要的疾病原因,但监测依赖运动员识别并报告症状。本研究旨在评估连续空气采样结合现场分子检测是否能在精英团队的集中活动空间内于比赛期间检测到呼吸道病毒核酸,以及由此产生的信号是否具有操作实用性。
设计与对象
我们开展了一项前瞻性描述性环境-监测研究,在2026年国际足联世界杯期间(2026年6月3日至7月4日)对加拿大国家男子足球队在五个宿主城市的活动进行了追踪。InBio Apollo生物气溶胶采样器在每家酒店的最多四间球队指定房间内连续运行,包括理疗室、餐厅、器材室以及教练室或走廊。
核心方法
滤器每天大约更换两次,在现场进行洗脱,并使用Cepheid Xpert® Xpress SARS-CoV-2/Flu/RSV plus试剂盒进行检测。若任一靶标的循环阈值(Ct)值报告为小于45,则样本判定为阳性。
主要结果
在174个空气过滤器中,检测到13例病毒遗传物质(9例SARS-CoV-2、3例甲型流感病毒、1例乙型流感病毒、0例呼吸道合胞病毒)。检测结果在锦标赛早期稀疏,晚期集中。一名球员因发热被送回家当天早晨出现甲型流感病毒信号,而SARS-CoV-2信号与酒店可视患病员工同时出现,在排除患病员工后信号消退。
解释与创新
在精英团队的移动环境中,通过即时检测进行空气采样是可行的,并能在比赛期间发现与行为无关的病毒信号,这可能为更早采取预防措施提供机会。
科研与公卫意义
空气采样提供了一种候选的行为无关方法,可生成早期预警信号,使团队能够有选择地加强防护措施,从而最大限度地减少运动员及团队工作人员的病毒暴露风险。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据未报告该研究的局限性或证据强度。

Air sampling in team congregate spaces for early detection of respiratory virus threats at the 2026 FIFA World CupTM

Authors: Simon, D., Locksmith, T. J., Minor, N. R., Emmen, I. E., Wilson, N. A., OConnor, E. J., OConnor, S. L., OConnor, D. H.
Original abstract (English)
Objective.Respiratory infections are the leading cause of illness at major sporting events, yet surveillance relies on athletes recognising and reporting symptoms. We evaluated whether continuous air sampling with point-of-care molecular testing could detect respiratory-virus nucleic acids in an elite teams congregate spaces during competition, and whether the resulting signals were operationally useful. Methods.We performed a prospective, descriptive environmental-surveillance study following the Canadian mens national soccer team across five host cities during the 2026 FIFA World Cup (3 June to 4 July 2026). InBio Apollo bioaerosol samplers ran continuously in up to four teamdesignated rooms per hotel (physiotherapy, meal, equipment, and coaches room or hallway). Filters were changed approximately twice daily, eluted on-site, and tested with the Cepheid Xpert(R) Xpress SARS-CoV-2/Flu/RSV plus assay. A sample was considered positive if any cycle-threshold (Ct) value was reported, as less than 45, for a target. Results.Of 174 air filters, there were 13 detections of virus genetic material (9 SARS-CoV-2, 3 influenza A virus, 1 influenza B virus, 0 RSV). Detections were sparse early and clustered late in the tournament. An influenza A signal appeared the morning a player was sent home febrile, and SARS-CoV-2 signals coincided with visibly ill hotel staff, with signals falling after ill staff were excluded. Conclusion.Air sampling with point-of-care testing is feasible in the mobile environment of an elite team and can surface behavior-independent viral signals during competition that may offer opportunities for earlier precautionary actions. Summary BoxO_ST_ABSWhat is already known on this topicC_ST_ABSRespiratory infections are the leading cause of illnesses at major sporting events, and conventional surveillance depends on athletes recognising and reporting symptoms, which misses presymptomatic, mild, and asymptomatic infections. Air sampling can detect respiratory viruses in congregate settings but has not been evaluated in elite team housing during competition. What this study addsContinuous air sampling with same-day point-of-care testing was feasible across a five-city World Cup campaign and detected SARS-CoV-2, influenza A, and influenza B in shared team spaces even during the low-circulation Northern-hemisphere summer, with several signals coinciding with a febrile player and with visibly ill staff. How this study might affect research, practice or policyAir sampling offers a candidate behavior-independent method to generate an early-warning signal that could allow teams to escalate precautions selectively, minimizing virus exposures among athletes and team staff. Central Figure O_FIG O_LINKSMALLFIG WIDTH=200 HEIGHT=157 SRC="FIGDIR/small/26360542v1_ufig1.gif" ALT="Figure 1"> View larger version (44K): org.highwire.dtl.DTLVardef@1bc3ae7org.highwire.dtl.DTLVardef@bf47dforg.highwire.dtl.DTLVardef@7965forg.highwire.dtl.DTLVardef@214a7a_HPS_FORMAT_FIGEXP M_FIG Air sampling for early detection of respiratory-virus threats in an elite team during competition. Continuous bioaerosol sampling was performed in up to four team congregate spaces per hotel, typically the meal, equipment, physiotherapy, and coaches rooms, across five host cities during the 2026 FIFA World Cup. Each samplers filter was eluted on-site and tested on a Cepheid GeneXpert SARS-CoV-2/Flu/RSV plus cartridge, giving a same-day, point-of-care result (top). Viral genetic material was detected in team-hotel air in every one of the five cities. The timeline shows sampling coverage as a horizontal band and each viral detection as a vertical tick on a single continuous time axis across the tournament, with detections sparse early and clustered in the final cities (middle). A positive air signal can trigger low-cost, low-regret responses before an outbreak is clinically apparent, such as masking, portable air purifiers, distancing and reduced occupancy, far-UVC lighting, reinforced ventilation, and excluding visibly ill personnel (bottom).
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Respiratory infections are the leading cause of illness at major sporting events, yet surveillance relies on athletes recognising and reporting symptoms. The study aimed to evaluate whether continuous air sampling with point-of-care molecular testing could detect respiratory-virus nucleic acids in an elite team's congregate spaces during competition and whether the resulting signals were operationally useful.
Design and population
We performed a prospective, descriptive environmental-surveillance study following the Canadian men's national soccer team across five host cities during the 2026 FIFA World Cup (3 June to 4 July 2026). InBio Apollo bioaerosol samplers ran continuously in up to four team-designated rooms per hotel, including physiotherapy, meal, equipment, and coaches rooms or hallways.
Core methods
Filters were changed approximately twice daily, eluted on-site, and tested with the Cepheid Xpert(R) Xpress SARS-CoV-2/Flu/RSV plus assay. A sample was considered positive if any cycle-threshold (Ct) value was reported, as less than 45, for a target.
Main results
Of 174 air filters, there were 13 detections of virus genetic material (9 SARS-CoV-2, 3 influenza A virus, 1 influenza B virus, 0 RSV). Detections were sparse early and clustered late in the tournament. An influenza A signal appeared the morning a player was sent home febrile, and SARS-CoV-2 signals coincided with visibly ill hotel staff, with signals falling after ill staff were excluded.
Interpretation and novelty
Air sampling with point-of-care testing is feasible in the mobile environment of an elite team and can surface behavior-independent viral signals during competition that may offer opportunities for earlier precautionary actions.
Scientific and public-health significance
Air sampling offers a candidate behavior-independent method to generate an early-warning signal that could allow teams to escalate precautions selectively, minimizing virus exposures among athletes and team staff.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations or evidence strength for the study.
